### Task 4: The gate: kinds, sites, risky acts, passwords, the card and the line

`gate.rs` is the pure decision of section 3: the kind of each tool of the pinned server (and of any other name), the dot's `Browser`/`BrowserRisky` rules read into `SiteRules`, and `judge`, which allows, asks with the data of the approval card (`Ask`) or refuses with a sentence. It also gives the rules a card offers (wired into `policy::allow_candidates`, so `decide_approval` accepts them unchanged) and the line the run view shows for a performed call.

**Files:**

- Create: `crates/dots-core/src/browser/gate.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dots-core/src/browser/mod.rs` (the module list, the server's name)
- Modify: `crates/dots-core/src/policy.rs` (`allow_candidates` near line 703)
- Test: `crates/dots-core/tests/browser_gate_test.rs` (new)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `sites::{site_of, check_site, rule_site, site_matches, place}` (Task 2); `snapshot::{Element, RefMap}` (Task 3); `policy::{Action, Policy, RuleCandidate}`.
- Produces:
  - In `dots_core::browser`: `pub const SERVER: &str = "browser"`, `pub const PREFIX: &str = "mcp__browser__"`, `pub fn is_browser_tool(qualified: &str) -> bool`.
  - In `dots_core::browser::gate`: `RISKY_WORDS: [&str; 23]`, `SECRET_WORDS: [&str; 7]`, `PASSWORD_REFUSAL`, `ASK_KEY = "dots_browser"`, `READ_TOOLS: [&str; 10]`; `enum Act { Click, Type, Fill, Select, Press, Hover, Drag, Drop }` and `enum Danger { RunCode, Upload, Network, Other }` (each with `as_str`); `enum Kind { Read, Open { url }, Act { act, targets, typed, key }, AcceptDialog, Dangerous(Danger) }`; `pub fn classify(tool: &str, input: &Value) -> Kind`; `struct SiteRules` with `fn of(policy: &Policy) -> SiteRules`, `denied`, `approved`, `risky_allowed`, `risky_denied` (each `(&self, site: &str) -> bool`); `#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)] struct Ask { action: String, site: Option<String>, page: Option<String>, labels: Vec<Option<String>>, risky: bool, dangerous: bool }`; `enum Verdict { Allow, Ask(Ask), Refuse(String) }`; `pub fn judge(tool: &str, kind: &Kind, page: Option<&str>, refs: Option<&RefMap>, rules: &SiteRules, dot_name: &str) -> Verdict`; `pub fn line(tool: &str, kind: &Kind, refs: Option<&RefMap>) -> Option<String>`; `pub fn rule_candidates(tool: &str, input: &Value) -> Option<Vec<RuleCandidate>>`; `pub fn has_word(text: &str, words: &[&str]) -> bool`; `pub fn is_risky(element: &Element) -> bool`; `pub fn is_secret(element: &Element) -> bool`.
  - `policy::allow_candidates` answers `rule_candidates` for a browser tool.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create `crates/dots-core/tests/browser_gate_test.rs`:**

```rust
//! The browser gate (browser spec section 3), pure: each tool's kind, sites and rules, risky
//! words, targets that are not references of the current snapshot, refused passwords, deny
//! over allow, what a card stores and offers, and the run view's line, on the references of a
//! captured snapshot of a cart page served as `https://shop.com/cart`.

mod common;

use common::playwright::{on, SNAPSHOT};
use dots_core::browser::gate::{
    classify, has_word, is_risky, is_secret, judge, line, rule_candidates, Act, Ask, Danger,
    Kind, SiteRules, Verdict, ASK_KEY, PASSWORD_REFUSAL, RISKY_WORDS, SECRET_WORDS,
};
use dots_core::browser::snapshot::{read_snapshot, Element, RefMap};
use dots_core::policy::{allow_candidates, Action, Policy, Preset, Rule, RuleCandidate};
use serde_json::{json, Value};

const CART: &str = "https://shop.com/cart";
const SHOP: (&str, Action) = ("Browser(shop.com)", Action::Allow);
const RISKY: (&str, Action) = ("BrowserRisky(shop.com)", Action::Allow);
const EVERYTHING: [(&str, Action); 2] = [
    ("Browser(*)", Action::Allow),
    ("BrowserRisky(*)", Action::Allow),
];

fn refs() -> RefMap {
    read_snapshot(&on(SNAPSHOT, CART)).unwrap()
}

fn rules(list: &[(&str, Action)]) -> SiteRules {
    let mut p = Policy::preset(Preset::Sandboxed);
    p.rules
        .extend(list.iter().map(|(tool, action)| Rule::new(tool, *action)));
    SiteRules::of(&p)
}

/// The verdict on `tool` with `input` on the cart page, under the rules `list`.
fn verdict(tool: &str, input: Value, list: &[(&str, Action)]) -> Verdict {
    judge(
        tool,
        &classify(tool, &input),
        Some(CART),
        Some(&refs()),
        &rules(list),
        "shopper",
    )
}

fn asks(v: &Verdict) -> &Ask {
    match v {
        Verdict::Ask(ask) => ask,
        other => panic!("expected an ask, got {other:?}"),
    }
}

fn denied(site: &str) -> Verdict {
    Verdict::Refuse(format!(
        "Denied by the policy of dot 'shopper': the browser may not be used on {site}."
    ))
}

fn s(text: &str) -> String {
    text.to_string()
}

#[test]
fn every_tool_of_the_pinned_server_has_its_kind() {
    for tool in [
        "browser_snapshot",
        "browser_take_screenshot",
        "browser_wait_for",
        "browser_navigate_back",
        "browser_console_messages",
        "browser_find",
        "browser_resize",
        "browser_emulate_media",
        "browser_close",
    ] {
        assert_eq!(classify(tool, &json!({})), Kind::Read, "{tool}");
    }
    let open = |url: &str| Kind::Open { url: s(url) };
    assert_eq!(classify("browser_tabs", &json!({"action": "list"})), Kind::Read);
    assert_eq!(classify("browser_tabs", &json!({"action": "new"})), Kind::Read);
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_tabs", &json!({"action": "new", "url": "https://a.com/"})),
        open("https://a.com/")
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_tabs", &json!({"action": "select", "index": 0, "url": "https://a.com/"})),
        Kind::Read
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_navigate", &json!({"url": "https://a.com/"})),
        open("https://a.com/")
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_navigate", &json!({"url": " JavaScript:alert(1)"})),
        Kind::Dangerous(Danger::RunCode)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_handle_dialog", &json!({"accept": true})),
        Kind::AcceptDialog
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_handle_dialog", &json!({"accept": false})),
        Kind::Read
    );
    let act = |act: Act, targets: &[&str], typed: &[&str], key: Option<&str>| Kind::Act {
        act,
        targets: targets.iter().map(|t| s(t)).collect(),
        typed: typed.iter().map(|t| s(t)).collect(),
        key: key.map(s),
    };
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_click", &json!({"target": "e16", "element": "Pay"})),
        act(Act::Click, &["e16"], &[], None)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_type", &json!({"target": "e11", "text": "shoes"})),
        act(Act::Type, &["e11"], &["e11"], None)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify(
            "browser_fill_form",
            &json!({"fields": [
                {"target": "e11", "name": "Search", "type": "textbox", "value": "x"},
                {"target": "e20", "name": "Remember", "type": "checkbox", "value": "true"},
                {"target": "e15", "name": "Code", "value": "1"}
            ]})
        ),
        act(Act::Fill, &["e11", "e20", "e15"], &["e11", "e15"], None)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_select_option", &json!({"target": "e19", "values": ["Large"]})),
        act(Act::Select, &["e19"], &[], None)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_press_key", &json!({"key": "Enter"})),
        act(Act::Press, &[], &[], Some("Enter"))
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_hover", &json!({"target": "e6"})),
        act(Act::Hover, &["e6"], &[], None)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_drag", &json!({"startTarget": "e6", "endTarget": "e21"})),
        act(Act::Drag, &["e6", "e21"], &[], None)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_drop", &json!({"target": "e1", "data": {"text/plain": "hi"}})),
        act(Act::Drop, &["e1"], &[], None)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_drop", &json!({"target": "e1", "paths": []})),
        act(Act::Drop, &["e1"], &[], None)
    );
    // Dropping files sends files from the PC, as an upload does.
    assert_eq!(
        classify("browser_drop", &json!({"target": "e1", "paths": ["C:/a.pdf"]})),
        Kind::Dangerous(Danger::Upload)
    );
    for (tool, danger) in [
        ("browser_evaluate", Danger::RunCode),
        ("browser_run_code_unsafe", Danger::RunCode),
        ("browser_file_upload", Danger::Upload),
        ("browser_network_request", Danger::Network),
        ("browser_network_requests", Danger::Network),
        ("browser_install", Danger::Other),
        ("checkout_now", Danger::Other),
    ] {
        assert_eq!(classify(tool, &json!({})), Kind::Dangerous(danger), "{tool}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn reading_is_free_unless_the_site_is_denied() {
    assert_eq!(verdict("browser_snapshot", json!({}), &[]), Verdict::Allow);
    assert_eq!(
        verdict("browser_take_screenshot", json!({"scale": "css"}), &[]),
        Verdict::Allow
    );
    assert_eq!(
        verdict(
            "browser_snapshot",
            json!({}),
            &[SHOP, ("Browser(shop.com)", Action::Deny)]
        ),
        denied("shop.com")
    );
    // A page with no site is not covered by `*`.
    assert_eq!(
        judge(
            "browser_snapshot",
            &Kind::Read,
            Some("about:blank"),
            None,
            &rules(&[("Browser(*)", Action::Deny)]),
            "shopper"
        ),
        Verdict::Allow
    );
}

#[test]
fn opening_a_page_asks_unless_its_site_is_approved() {
    let open = |url: &str, list: &[(&str, Action)]| {
        verdict("browser_navigate", json!({"url": url}), list)
    };
    assert_eq!(open("https://shop.com/cart", &[SHOP]), Verdict::Allow);
    assert_eq!(open("https://www.SHOP.com:443/x", &[SHOP]), Verdict::Allow);
    assert_eq!(open("https://app.shop.com/", &[SHOP]), Verdict::Allow);
    assert_eq!(open("https://shop.com/", &[RISKY]), Verdict::Allow);
    assert_eq!(open("https://any.example/", &EVERYTHING), Verdict::Allow);
    assert_eq!(
        asks(&open("https://new.example/a?b=1", &[SHOP])),
        &Ask {
            action: s("open"),
            site: Some(s("new.example")),
            page: Some(s("new.example/a")),
            labels: vec![],
            risky: false,
            dangerous: false,
        }
    );
    assert_eq!(
        asks(&open("https://evilshop.com/", &[SHOP])).site.as_deref(),
        Some("evilshop.com")
    );
    for odd in [
        "https://shop.com@evil.com/",
        "https://evil.com\\@shop.com/",
        "https://shop%2ecom/",
        "shop.com/cart",
        "data:text/html,hi",
        "file:///C:/a.html",
        "about:blank",
        "edge://settings/",
    ] {
        assert_eq!(asks(&open(odd, &EVERYTHING)).site, None, "{odd}");
    }
    // A `javascript:` address runs code in the page: no rule allows it.
    let script = open("javascript:document.forms[0].submit()", &EVERYTHING);
    assert!(asks(&script).dangerous);
    // Deny wins over allow, and ask over allow.
    assert_eq!(
        open("https://shop.com/", &[SHOP, ("Browser(shop.com)", Action::Deny)]),
        denied("shop.com")
    );
    let bank = [("Browser(*)", Action::Allow), ("Browser(bank.com)", Action::Ask)];
    assert!(matches!(open("https://bank.com/", &bank), Verdict::Ask(_)));
    assert_eq!(open("https://shop.com/", &bank), Verdict::Allow);
}

#[test]
fn acting_needs_an_approved_site_and_a_risky_act_asks_there() {
    assert_eq!(verdict("browser_click", json!({"target": "e6"}), &[SHOP]), Verdict::Allow);
    assert_eq!(
        verdict("browser_type", json!({"target": "e11", "text": "shoes"}), &[SHOP]),
        Verdict::Allow
    );
    // `Enter` is not risky on its own (spec section 11).
    assert_eq!(
        verdict("browser_press_key", json!({"key": "Enter"}), &[SHOP]),
        Verdict::Allow
    );
    // The model's own description of the element plays no part.
    assert_eq!(
        asks(&verdict(
            "browser_click",
            json!({"target": "e6", "element": "Pay now!"}),
            &[]
        )),
        &Ask {
            action: s("click"),
            site: Some(s("shop.com")),
            page: Some(s("shop.com/cart")),
            labels: vec![Some(s("Home"))],
            risky: false,
            dangerous: false,
        }
    );
    assert_eq!(
        asks(&verdict(
            "browser_click",
            json!({"target": "e16", "element": "Home link"}),
            &[SHOP]
        )),
        &Ask {
            action: s("click"),
            site: Some(s("shop.com")),
            page: Some(s("shop.com/cart")),
            labels: vec![Some(s("Pay now"))],
            risky: true,
            dangerous: false,
        }
    );
    assert_eq!(
        verdict("browser_click", json!({"target": "e16"}), &[SHOP, RISKY]),
        Verdict::Allow
    );
    // An allow `BrowserRisky` approves the site as well.
    assert_eq!(verdict("browser_click", json!({"target": "e16"}), &[RISKY]), Verdict::Allow);
    // "My orders" is a plain act: `orders` is not the whole word `order`.
    assert_eq!(verdict("browser_click", json!({"target": "e8"}), &[SHOP]), Verdict::Allow);
    // "Delete account" is risky.
    assert!(asks(&verdict("browser_click", json!({"target": "e18"}), &[SHOP])).risky);
    // A `BrowserRisky` deny refuses the risky acts; an ask rule keeps the site asking.
    assert!(matches!(
        verdict(
            "browser_click",
            json!({"target": "e16"}),
            &[SHOP, RISKY, ("BrowserRisky(shop.com)", Action::Deny)]
        ),
        Verdict::Refuse(_)
    ));
    assert!(matches!(
        verdict(
            "browser_click",
            json!({"target": "e6"}),
            &[("Browser(*)", Action::Allow), ("Browser(shop.com)", Action::Ask)]
        ),
        Verdict::Ask(_)
    ));
    // Accepting a dialog is always risky; dismissing it is a read.
    let dialog = verdict("browser_handle_dialog", json!({"accept": true}), &[SHOP]);
    assert_eq!((asks(&dialog).action.as_str(), asks(&dialog).risky), ("dialog", true));
    assert_eq!(
        verdict("browser_handle_dialog", json!({"accept": true}), &[SHOP, RISKY]),
        Verdict::Allow
    );
    assert_eq!(
        verdict("browser_handle_dialog", json!({"accept": false}), &[]),
        Verdict::Allow
    );
    // A drag is risky when either end is.
    assert!(
        asks(&verdict(
            "browser_drag",
            json!({"startTarget": "e6", "endTarget": "e18"}),
            &[SHOP]
        ))
        .risky
    );
    // An act on a page with no site, or on a page Dots could not confirm, asks.
    let click_home = classify("browser_click", &json!({"target": "e6"}));
    for page in [Some("about:blank"), None] {
        let v = judge("browser_click", &click_home, page, Some(&refs()), &rules(&EVERYTHING), "shopper");
        assert_eq!(asks(&v).site, None, "{page:?}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn risky_words_are_whole_words_in_any_case_and_spacing() {
    for risky in [
        "PAY NOW",
        "Pay-now",
        "Buy\u{a0}now",
        "Checkout →",
        "Confirm & pay",
        "Place order",
        "Sign in",
        "Post",
        "submit",
        "Delete",
        "Book a table",
        "Remove item (2)",
        "unsubscribe",
        "Step 2: transfer",
    ] {
        assert!(has_word(risky, &RISKY_WORDS), "{risky}");
    }
    for plain in [
        "Paypal",
        "Placeholder",
        "Ordering",
        "Postcode",
        "Display",
        "Sender",
        "Bookmarks",
        "Cancellation policy",
        "",
    ] {
        assert!(!has_word(plain, &RISKY_WORDS), "{plain}");
    }
    // The role counts as well as the name.
    assert!(is_risky(&Element::new("link", "Checkout")));
    assert!(is_risky(&Element::new("menuitem-delete", "")));
    assert!(!is_risky(&Element::new("button", "Continue")));
}

#[test]
fn a_target_not_in_the_current_snapshot_is_risky() {
    for target in [
        "e999",
        "E16",
        "text=Pay",
        "role=button[name=\"Pay\"]",
        "#pay",
        "",
        "e6x",
    ] {
        let v = verdict("browser_click", json!({"target": target}), &[SHOP]);
        assert!(asks(&v).risky, "{target:?}");
        assert_eq!(asks(&v).labels, vec![None], "{target:?}");
    }
    // A known reference with spaces around it is that reference.
    assert_eq!(verdict("browser_click", json!({"target": " e6 "}), &[SHOP]), Verdict::Allow);
    let click_home = classify("browser_click", &json!({"target": "e6"}));
    // Before any snapshot of the page, and with another tab's references, nothing is known.
    for map in [None, Some(&RefMap::new())] {
        let v = judge("browser_click", &click_home, Some(CART), map, &rules(&[SHOP]), "shopper");
        assert!(asks(&v).risky);
    }
    // With risky actions allowed there, an unknown target passes too.
    assert_eq!(
        verdict("browser_click", json!({"target": "text=Pay"}), &[SHOP, RISKY]),
        Verdict::Allow
    );
}

#[test]
fn typing_a_password_or_code_is_refused_whatever_the_rules() {
    let refusal = Verdict::Refuse(s(PASSWORD_REFUSAL));
    assert_eq!(
        PASSWORD_REFUSAL,
        "Dots does not type passwords or codes. Ask the person to enter it in the browser window, with your ask tool."
    );
    assert_eq!(
        verdict("browser_type", json!({"target": "e13", "text": "hunter2"}), &EVERYTHING),
        refusal
    );
    assert_eq!(
        verdict("browser_type", json!({"target": "e15", "text": "123456"}), &EVERYTHING),
        refusal
    );
    assert_eq!(
        verdict(
            "browser_fill_form",
            json!({"fields": [
                {"target": "e11", "name": "Search", "type": "textbox", "value": "x"},
                {"target": "e13", "name": "Your name", "type": "textbox", "value": "y"}
            ]}),
            &EVERYTHING
        ),
        refusal
    );
    let field = |role: &str, name: &str| {
        let mut map = RefMap::new();
        map.insert(s("e1"), Element::new(role, name));
        let typing = classify("browser_type", &json!({"target": "e1", "text": "x"}));
        judge("browser_type", &typing, Some(CART), Some(&map), &rules(&EVERYTHING), "shopper")
    };
    for secret in [
        "Password",
        "Confirm password",
        "PIN",
        "Enter your PIN",
        "Passcode",
        "OTP",
        "One-time code",
        "one time password",
        "Verification code",
        "Security Code",
        "CVV / security code",
    ] {
        assert_eq!(field("textbox", secret), refusal, "{secret}");
    }
    assert_eq!(field("password", ""), refusal);
    for plain in [
        "Pinterest handle",
        "Spin speed",
        "Shipping",
        "Passport number",
        "Verification",
        "Code",
    ] {
        assert_eq!(field("textbox", plain), Verdict::Allow, "{plain}");
    }
    assert!(has_word("Your one-time passcode", &SECRET_WORDS));
    assert!(is_secret(&Element::new("textbox", "PIN code")));
    // A secret field ticked, not typed into, is an ordinary act.
    assert_eq!(
        verdict(
            "browser_fill_form",
            json!({"fields": [{"target": "e13", "name": "x", "type": "checkbox", "value": "true"}]}),
            &EVERYTHING
        ),
        Verdict::Allow
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_dangerous_tool_always_asks_and_offers_no_rule() {
    for (tool, action) in [
        ("browser_evaluate", "run_code"),
        ("browser_run_code_unsafe", "run_code"),
        ("browser_file_upload", "upload"),
        ("browser_network_requests", "network"),
        ("browser_network_request", "network"),
    ] {
        let ask = asks(&verdict(tool, json!({}), &EVERYTHING)).clone();
        assert_eq!(ask.action, action, "{tool}");
        assert!(ask.dangerous && ask.risky, "{tool}");
        assert_eq!(ask.site.as_deref(), Some("shop.com"), "{tool}");
        let input = json!({ ASK_KEY: ask });
        assert_eq!(
            rule_candidates(&format!("mcp__browser__{tool}"), &input),
            Some(vec![]),
            "{tool}"
        );
    }
    let other = asks(&verdict("checkout_now", json!({}), &EVERYTHING)).clone();
    assert_eq!(other.action, "other");
    assert_eq!(other.labels, vec![Some(s("checkout_now"))]);
    assert_eq!(
        verdict("browser_evaluate", json!({}), &[("Browser(shop.com)", Action::Deny)]),
        denied("shop.com")
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_card_offers_the_rules_of_its_site() {
    let pay = asks(&verdict("browser_click", json!({"target": "e16"}), &[SHOP])).clone();
    let both = vec![
        RuleCandidate {
            pattern: s("Browser(shop.com)"),
            label: s("always allow on shop.com"),
        },
        RuleCandidate {
            pattern: s("BrowserRisky(shop.com)"),
            label: s("always allow risky actions on shop.com"),
        },
    ];
    let input = json!({"target": "e16", ASK_KEY: pay});
    assert_eq!(rule_candidates("mcp__browser__browser_click", &input), Some(both.clone()));
    assert_eq!(allow_candidates("mcp__browser__browser_click", &input, None), both);
    let home = asks(&verdict("browser_click", json!({"target": "e6"}), &[])).clone();
    assert_eq!(
        allow_candidates("mcp__browser__browser_click", &json!({ ASK_KEY: home.clone() }), None),
        both[..1].to_vec()
    );
    // No site, `*`, a site Dots would not save, or no card: nothing to save, and never a rule
    // for the whole server.
    for site in [None, Some(s("*")), Some(s("Shop.com"))] {
        let card = Ask { site, ..home.clone() };
        assert!(
            allow_candidates("mcp__browser__browser_click", &json!({ ASK_KEY: card }), None)
                .is_empty()
        );
    }
    assert!(allow_candidates("mcp__browser__browser_click", &json!({"target": "e6"}), None).is_empty());
    // Other tools keep their own rules.
    assert_eq!(rule_candidates("mcp__files__read", &json!({})), None);
    assert_eq!(allow_candidates("mcp__files__read", &json!({}), None).len(), 2);
}

#[test]
fn the_run_view_line_says_what_was_done() {
    let map = refs();
    let line_of = |tool: &str, input: Value| line(tool, &classify(tool, &input), Some(&map));
    let cases: [(&str, Value, Option<&str>); 12] = [
        ("browser_navigate", json!({"url": "https://www.shop.com/cart?x=1"}), Some("Opened shop.com/cart")),
        ("browser_click", json!({"target": "e16"}), Some("Clicked \"Pay now\"")),
        ("browser_type", json!({"target": "e11", "text": "shoes"}), Some("Typed into \"Search\"")),
        ("browser_click", json!({"target": "text=Pay"}), Some("Clicked text=Pay")),
        ("browser_take_screenshot", json!({"scale": "css"}), Some("Took a screenshot")),
        ("browser_snapshot", json!({}), Some("Read the page")),
        ("browser_press_key", json!({"key": "Enter"}), Some("Pressed Enter")),
        ("browser_fill_form", json!({"fields": [{"target": "e11"}, {"target": "e20"}]}), Some("Filled in 2 fields")),
        ("browser_drag", json!({"startTarget": "e6", "endTarget": "e21"}), Some("Dragged \"Home\" to \"Download statement\"")),
        ("browser_handle_dialog", json!({"accept": true}), Some("Accepted a dialog")),
        ("browser_evaluate", json!({"function": "() => 1"}), Some("Used browser_evaluate")),
        ("browser_tabs", json!({"action": "list"}), None),
    ];
    for (tool, input, want) in cases {
        assert_eq!(line_of(tool, input).as_deref(), want, "{tool}");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the gate and offer its rules**

**In `crates/dots-core/src/browser/mod.rs`, replace:**


**with:**

```rust
pub mod gate;
pub mod sites;
pub mod snapshot;

/// The built-in server's name (browser spec section 2.2): its tools are `mcp__browser__<tool>`
/// on both engines.
pub const SERVER: &str = "browser";
/// The prefix of its tools' names.
pub const PREFIX: &str = "mcp__browser__";

/// Whether `qualified` names a tool of the built-in browser.
pub fn is_browser_tool(qualified: &str) -> bool {
    qualified.len() > PREFIX.len() && qualified.starts_with(PREFIX)
}
```

**Create `crates/dots-core/src/browser/gate.rs`:**

```rust
//! The browser gate (browser spec section 3): pure code that decides each call of a `browser`
//! tool before the dot's other rules, from the call, the references of the page it acts on
//! (`snapshot::PageMemory`) and that page's address, which `RunBrowser` confirms in Edge. It
//! also writes what an approval card shows (`Ask`), the rules the card offers, and the line the
//! run view shows for a performed call.

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;

use super::sites::{check_site, place, rule_site, site_matches, site_of};
use super::snapshot::{Element, RefMap};
use crate::policy::{Action, Policy, RuleCandidate};

/// An act is risky when its target's name or role holds one of these as a whole word (section
/// 3.4).
pub const RISKY_WORDS: [&str; 23] = [
    "pay",
    "payment",
    "buy",
    "purchase",
    "checkout",
    "order",
    "send",
    "delete",
    "remove",
    "submit",
    "transfer",
    "confirm",
    "publish",
    "post",
    "sign",
    "subscribe",
    "unsubscribe",
    "cancel",
    "withdraw",
    "donate",
    "book",
    "reserve",
    "place",
];

/// Typing into an element whose name holds one of these as a whole word is refused (section
/// 3.5).
pub const SECRET_WORDS: [&str; 7] = [
    "password",
    "passcode",
    "pin",
    "one-time",
    "otp",
    "verification code",
    "security code",
];

/// The answer to typing a password or a code. No rule allows it.
pub const PASSWORD_REFUSAL: &str = "Dots does not type passwords or codes. Ask the person to enter it in the browser window, with your ask tool.";

/// The key under which an approval's input carries its card (`Ask`). Dots writes it; a key of
/// this name in the model's own input is dropped before anything else reads the input.
pub const ASK_KEY: &str = "dots_browser";

/// The read tools of the table in section 3.1. `browser_tabs` is one unless it opens a URL,
/// and `browser_handle_dialog` is one when it dismisses the dialog.
pub const READ_TOOLS: [&str; 10] = [
    "browser_snapshot",
    "browser_take_screenshot",
    "browser_wait_for",
    "browser_navigate_back",
    "browser_console_messages",
    "browser_find",
    "browser_resize",
    "browser_emulate_media",
    "browser_close",
    "browser_tabs",
];

/// What an act does to the page.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Act {
    Click,
    Type,
    Fill,
    Select,
    Press,
    Hover,
    Drag,
    Drop,
}

impl Act {
    /// The card's word for it.
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Act::Click => "click",
            Act::Type => "type",
            Act::Fill => "fill",
            Act::Select => "select",
            Act::Press => "press",
            Act::Hover => "hover",
            Act::Drag => "drag",
            Act::Drop => "drop",
        }
    }
}

/// What a dangerous call does: no rule allows it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Danger {
    /// Runs code in the page: `browser_evaluate`, `browser_run_code_unsafe`, a `javascript:`
    /// address.
    RunCode,
    /// Sends files from the PC: `browser_file_upload`, `browser_drop` with `paths`.
    Upload,
    /// Reads the page's network traffic.
    Network,
    /// A tool the table does not name.
    Other,
}

impl Danger {
    /// The card's word for it.
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Danger::RunCode => "run_code",
            Danger::Upload => "upload",
            Danger::Network => "network",
            Danger::Other => "other",
        }
    }
}

/// The kind of a call (section 3.1).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Kind {
    Read,
    /// Goes to `url`: `browser_navigate`, and `browser_tabs` `new` with a URL.
    Open { url: String },
    /// Acts on the current page: on the elements `targets` (references or selectors, as the
    /// model gave them), typing into `typed`; `key` for `browser_press_key`.
    Act {
        act: Act,
        targets: Vec<String>,
        typed: Vec<String>,
        key: Option<String>,
    },
    /// Accepts the page's dialog: always risky.
    AcceptDialog,
    Dangerous(Danger),
}

/// The kind of a call of `tool` (the name without `mcp__browser__`) with `input`.
pub fn classify(tool: &str, input: &Value) -> Kind {
    let text = |key: &str| {
        input
            .get(key)
            .and_then(Value::as_str)
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .to_string()
    };
    let act = |act: Act, targets: Vec<String>, typed: Vec<String>| Kind::Act {
        act,
        targets,
        typed,
        key: None,
    };
    match tool {
        "browser_navigate" => open(text("url")),
        "browser_tabs"
            if input.get("action").and_then(Value::as_str) == Some("new")
                && !text("url").trim().is_empty() =>
        {
            open(text("url"))
        }
        "browser_handle_dialog" => {
            if input.get("accept").and_then(Value::as_bool) == Some(true) {
                Kind::AcceptDialog
            } else {
                Kind::Read
            }
        }
        "browser_drop"
            if input
                .get("paths")
                .and_then(Value::as_array)
                .is_some_and(|paths| !paths.is_empty()) =>
        {
            Kind::Dangerous(Danger::Upload)
        }
        "browser_click" => act(Act::Click, vec![text("target")], Vec::new()),
        "browser_type" => act(Act::Type, vec![text("target")], vec![text("target")]),
        "browser_fill_form" => {
            let fields = input
                .get("fields")
                .and_then(Value::as_array)
                .cloned()
                .unwrap_or_default();
            let target = |field: &Value| {
                field
                    .get("target")
                    .and_then(Value::as_str)
                    .unwrap_or_default()
                    .to_string()
            };
            // A field without a type is taken as one typed into.
            let typed = fields
                .iter()
                .filter(|f| {
                    f.get("type")
                        .and_then(Value::as_str)
                        .is_none_or(|t| t == "textbox")
                })
                .map(target)
                .collect();
            act(Act::Fill, fields.iter().map(target).collect(), typed)
        }
        "browser_select_option" => act(Act::Select, vec![text("target")], Vec::new()),
        "browser_press_key" => Kind::Act {
            act: Act::Press,
            targets: Vec::new(),
            typed: Vec::new(),
            key: Some(text("key")),
        },
        "browser_hover" => act(Act::Hover, vec![text("target")], Vec::new()),
        "browser_drag" => act(
            Act::Drag,
            vec![text("startTarget"), text("endTarget")],
            Vec::new(),
        ),
        "browser_drop" => act(Act::Drop, vec![text("target")], Vec::new()),
        "browser_evaluate" | "browser_run_code_unsafe" => Kind::Dangerous(Danger::RunCode),
        "browser_file_upload" => Kind::Dangerous(Danger::Upload),
        "browser_network_request" | "browser_network_requests" => {
            Kind::Dangerous(Danger::Network)
        }
        tool if READ_TOOLS.contains(&tool) => Kind::Read,
        _ => Kind::Dangerous(Danger::Other),
    }
}

/// Going to `url`; a `javascript:` address runs code in the page instead.
fn open(url: String) -> Kind {
    if url
        .trim_start()
        .to_ascii_lowercase()
        .starts_with("javascript:")
    {
        Kind::Dangerous(Danger::RunCode)
    } else {
        Kind::Open { url }
    }
}

/// A dot's browser rules by what they say (section 6): which sites are allowed, asked,
/// denied, and where risky acts are allowed or denied. A rule whose site is not valid is
/// ignored (`Policy::validate` refuses it when the dot is saved).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SiteRules {
    allowed: Vec<String>,
    asked: Vec<String>,
    denied: Vec<String>,
    risky_allowed: Vec<String>,
    risky_denied: Vec<String>,
}

impl SiteRules {
    pub fn of(policy: &Policy) -> SiteRules {
        let mut rules = SiteRules::default();
        for rule in &policy.rules {
            let Some((risky, site)) = browser_rule(&rule.tool) else {
                continue;
            };
            let list = match (risky, rule.action) {
                (false, Action::Allow) => &mut rules.allowed,
                (false, Action::Ask) => &mut rules.asked,
                (false, Action::Deny) => &mut rules.denied,
                (true, Action::Allow) => &mut rules.risky_allowed,
                (true, Action::Deny) => &mut rules.risky_denied,
                (true, Action::Ask) => continue,
            };
            list.push(site);
        }
        rules
    }

    fn covers(list: &[String], site: &str) -> bool {
        list.iter().any(|rule| site_matches(rule, site))
    }

    /// A deny rule covers `site`: every call there is refused.
    pub fn denied(&self, site: &str) -> bool {
        Self::covers(&self.denied, site)
    }

    /// Going to `site` and acting there need no approval: an allow rule covers it (an allow
    /// `BrowserRisky` too), and no ask rule does.
    pub fn approved(&self, site: &str) -> bool {
        !Self::covers(&self.asked, site)
            && (Self::covers(&self.allowed, site) || Self::covers(&self.risky_allowed, site))
    }

    /// A risky act on `site` needs no approval.
    pub fn risky_allowed(&self, site: &str) -> bool {
        self.approved(site)
            && Self::covers(&self.risky_allowed, site)
            && !self.risky_denied(site)
    }

    /// A `BrowserRisky` deny rule covers `site`: its risky acts are refused.
    pub fn risky_denied(&self, site: &str) -> bool {
        Self::covers(&self.risky_denied, site)
    }
}

/// `(risky, site)` of a rule `Browser(<site>)` (`false`) or `BrowserRisky(<site>)` (`true`).
fn browser_rule(pattern: &str) -> Option<(bool, String)> {
    let (name, rest) = pattern.trim().split_once('(')?;
    let site = rest.strip_suffix(')')?;
    let risky = match name {
        "Browser" => false,
        "BrowserRisky" => true,
        _ => return None,
    };
    check_site(site).is_ok().then(|| (risky, rule_site(site)))
}

/// What an approval card shows about a browser call that asks (section 3.6), stored in the
/// approval's input under `ASK_KEY`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Ask {
    /// `open`, an act (`click`, `type`, `fill`, `select`, `press`, `hover`, `drag`, `drop`),
    /// `dialog`, or a danger (`run_code`, `upload`, `network`, `other`).
    pub action: String,
    /// The site the call goes to or acts on; `None` for a page with no site.
    pub site: Option<String>,
    /// The page as the card shows it (`shop.com/cart`).
    pub page: Option<String>,
    /// The labels of the elements it acts on, in order; `None` for an element that is not a
    /// reference of the page's snapshot. For `press` the key, for `other` the tool.
    pub labels: Vec<Option<String>>,
    pub risky: bool,
    /// No rule can allow it.
    pub dangerous: bool,
}

/// The gate's answer.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Verdict {
    Allow,
    Ask(Ask),
    /// Not performed, for this reason.
    Refuse(String),
}

/// Decides a call of `tool` of `kind` on the page `page` (its address, as `RunBrowser`
/// confirmed it; `None` when it could not), whose snapshot references are `refs`, under the
/// dot's `rules`. Typing a password or a code is refused whatever the rules; then a deny rule
/// refuses; a dangerous call asks; reading is free; going to a site asks unless it is
/// approved; an act asks unless its page's site is approved and, for a risky act, risky acts
/// are allowed there. A target that is not a reference of `refs` is risky.
pub fn judge(
    tool: &str,
    kind: &Kind,
    page: Option<&str>,
    refs: Option<&RefMap>,
    rules: &SiteRules,
    dot_name: &str,
) -> Verdict {
    let current = page.and_then(site_of);
    let refused = |site: &str| {
        Verdict::Refuse(format!(
            "Denied by the policy of dot '{dot_name}': the browser may not be used on {site}."
        ))
    };
    let ask = |action: &str,
               site: Option<String>,
               labels: Vec<Option<String>>,
               risky: bool,
               dangerous: bool| {
        Verdict::Ask(Ask {
            action: action.to_string(),
            site,
            page: page.map(place),
            labels,
            risky,
            dangerous,
        })
    };
    let on_page = |action: &str, labels: Vec<Option<String>>, risky: bool| match current.as_deref()
    {
        None => ask(action, None, labels, risky, false),
        Some(site) if rules.denied(site) || (risky && rules.risky_denied(site)) => refused(site),
        Some(site) if rules.approved(site) && (!risky || rules.risky_allowed(site)) => {
            Verdict::Allow
        }
        Some(_) => ask(action, current.clone(), labels, risky, false),
    };
    match kind {
        Kind::Read => match current.as_deref() {
            Some(site) if rules.denied(site) => refused(site),
            _ => Verdict::Allow,
        },
        Kind::Open { url } => {
            let target = site_of(url);
            match target.as_deref() {
                Some(site) if rules.denied(site) => refused(site),
                Some(site) if rules.approved(site) => Verdict::Allow,
                _ => Verdict::Ask(Ask {
                    action: "open".to_string(),
                    site: target.clone(),
                    page: Some(place(url)),
                    labels: Vec::new(),
                    risky: false,
                    dangerous: false,
                }),
            }
        }
        Kind::Dangerous(danger) => match current.as_deref() {
            Some(site) if rules.denied(site) => refused(site),
            _ => {
                let labels = match danger {
                    Danger::Other => vec![Some(tool.to_string())],
                    _ => Vec::new(),
                };
                ask(danger.as_str(), current.clone(), labels, true, true)
            }
        },
        Kind::AcceptDialog => on_page("dialog", Vec::new(), true),
        Kind::Act {
            act,
            targets,
            typed,
            key,
        } => {
            let element = |target: &String| refs.and_then(|map| map.get(target.trim()));
            if typed.iter().filter_map(element).any(is_secret) {
                return Verdict::Refuse(PASSWORD_REFUSAL.to_string());
            }
            let labels = match act {
                Act::Press => vec![key.clone()],
                _ => targets
                    .iter()
                    .map(|t| element(t).map(|e| e.label().to_string()))
                    .collect(),
            };
            let risky = targets.iter().any(|t| element(t).is_none_or(is_risky));
            on_page(act.as_str(), labels, risky)
        }
    }
}

/// `text` as words: lower case, every run of characters that are not letters or digits one
/// space, with a space at each end.
fn words_of(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::from(" ");
    for c in text.chars() {
        if c.is_alphanumeric() {
            out.extend(c.to_lowercase());
        } else if !out.ends_with(' ') {
            out.push(' ');
        }
    }
    if !out.ends_with(' ') {
        out.push(' ');
    }
    out
}

/// Whether `text` holds one of `words` (a word or a phrase) as whole words, ignoring case.
pub fn has_word(text: &str, words: &[&str]) -> bool {
    let text = words_of(text);
    words.iter().any(|word| text.contains(&words_of(word)))
}

/// An act on `element` is risky (section 3.4).
pub fn is_risky(element: &Element) -> bool {
    has_word(&element.name, &RISKY_WORDS) || has_word(&element.role, &RISKY_WORDS)
}

/// `element` takes a password or a code (section 3.5).
pub fn is_secret(element: &Element) -> bool {
    has_word(&element.name, &SECRET_WORDS) || element.role.eq_ignore_ascii_case("password")
}

/// The rules a person may save while approving a browser call (section 3.6): "always allow on
/// <site>" and, for a risky act, "always allow risky actions on <site>". None for a dangerous
/// call, a page with no site, or an input without Dots' card. `None` for a tool that is not
/// the browser's.
pub fn rule_candidates(tool: &str, input: &Value) -> Option<Vec<RuleCandidate>> {
    if !super::is_browser_tool(tool) {
        return None;
    }
    let card = input
        .get(ASK_KEY)
        .cloned()
        .and_then(|v| serde_json::from_value::<Ask>(v).ok());
    let Some(Ask {
        site: Some(site),
        risky,
        dangerous: false,
        ..
    }) = card
    else {
        return Some(Vec::new());
    };
    if site == "*" || check_site(&site).is_err() {
        return Some(Vec::new());
    }
    let mut out = vec![RuleCandidate {
        pattern: format!("Browser({site})"),
        label: format!("always allow on {site}"),
    }];
    if risky {
        out.push(RuleCandidate {
            pattern: format!("BrowserRisky({site})"),
            label: format!("always allow risky actions on {site}"),
        });
    }
    Some(out)
}

/// The line the run view shows for a call Dots performed (section 7), from what the gate knew
/// when it decided: `Opened shop.com/cart`, `Clicked "Add to cart"`, `Typed into "Search"`,
/// `Took a screenshot`. An element that is not a reference shows as the model named it.
/// `None` for a call that needs no line of its own.
pub fn line(tool: &str, kind: &Kind, refs: Option<&RefMap>) -> Option<String> {
    let named = |target: &String| match refs.and_then(|map| map.get(target.trim())) {
        Some(element) => format!("\"{}\"", element.label()),
        None => target.trim().to_string(),
    };
    Some(match kind {
        Kind::Open { url } => format!("Opened {}", place(url)),
        Kind::AcceptDialog => "Accepted a dialog".to_string(),
        Kind::Dangerous(_) => format!("Used {tool}"),
        Kind::Read => match tool {
            "browser_snapshot" => "Read the page".to_string(),
            "browser_take_screenshot" => "Took a screenshot".to_string(),
            "browser_navigate_back" => "Went back".to_string(),
            _ => return None,
        },
        Kind::Act {
            act, targets, key, ..
        } => {
            let first = targets.first().map(named).unwrap_or_default();
            match act {
                Act::Click => format!("Clicked {first}"),
                Act::Type => format!("Typed into {first}"),
                Act::Fill if targets.len() == 1 => "Filled in 1 field".to_string(),
                Act::Fill => format!("Filled in {} fields", targets.len()),
                Act::Select => format!("Chose an option in {first}"),
                Act::Press => format!("Pressed {}", key.as_deref().unwrap_or_default()),
                Act::Hover => format!("Pointed at {first}"),
                Act::Drag => format!(
                    "Dragged {first} to {}",
                    targets.get(1).map(named).unwrap_or_default()
                ),
                Act::Drop => format!("Dropped onto {first}"),
            }
        }
    })
}
```

**In `crates/dots-core/src/policy.rs`, replace:**

```rust
pub fn allow_candidates(tool: &str, input: &Value, workspace: Option<&Path>) -> Vec<RuleCandidate> {
    let ws = workspace_key(workspace);
```

**with:**

```rust
pub fn allow_candidates(tool: &str, input: &Value, workspace: Option<&Path>) -> Vec<RuleCandidate> {
    // A browser call offers its site's rules and nothing else (browser spec section 3.6).
    if let Some(candidates) = crate::browser::gate::rule_candidates(tool, input) {
        return candidates;
    }
    let ws = workspace_key(workspace);
```

### Task 5: The host: one Edge per dot, its pages, and a fake Edge for tests

`host.rs` holds the `BrowserHost` trait and `EdgeHost`: it starts Edge on the dot's profile with a free port (after writing the dot's download folder into the profile), finds a running one by its port (kept in a file, so a Dots that starts again finds it), reads the addresses of its pages from `/json/list`, closes it with `/json/close`, refuses a profile another Edge holds, and removes a deleted dot's folder. It also finds Edge and `npx`, and moves finished downloads into a run's workspace. `fake-browser` stands in for Edge in every test: it holds the profile's lock file as Edge does and answers the same HTTP.

**Files:**

- Create: `crates/dots-core/src/browser/host.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dots-core/src/browser/mod.rs` (the module list)
- Create: `crates/dots-core/src/bin/fake-browser.rs`
- Create: `crates/dots-core/tests/common/fake_browser.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dots-core/tests/common/mod.rs` (the module list)
- Test: `crates/dots-core/tests/browser_host_test.rs` (new)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `mcp::resolve_program`, `proc::hide_window`; `reqwest`, `async_trait`, `thiserror` (dependencies already).
- Produces (in `dots_core::browser::host`):
  - `EDGE_FLAGS: [&str; 7]`, `START_WAIT` (20 s), `CLOSE_WAIT` (5 s).
  - `#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)] enum HostError { NotFound, ProfileInUse, Start(String), Unreachable(String) }` (the first two display the spec's sentences).
  - `#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)] struct Opened { port: u16, started: bool }`.
  - `#[async_trait] trait BrowserHost: Send + Sync { async fn open(&self, dot_id: &str, exe: &Path) -> Result<Opened, HostError>; async fn pages(&self, port: u16) -> Result<Vec<String>, HostError>; async fn close(&self, dot_id: &str); fn downloads(&self, dot_id: &str) -> PathBuf; async fn remove(&self, dot_id: &str); }`.
  - `struct EdgeHost` with `fn new(root: impl Into<PathBuf>) -> EdgeHost`, `fn dot_dir(&self, dot_id: &str) -> PathBuf`, `fn profile(&self, dot_id: &str) -> PathBuf`; `impl BrowserHost for EdgeHost`.
  - `pub fn edge_args(profile: &Path, port: u16) -> Vec<String>`; `pub fn page_urls(targets: &[Value]) -> Vec<String>`; `pub fn edge_candidates(configured: Option<&str>, env: &dyn Fn(&str) -> Option<String>) -> Vec<PathBuf>`; `pub fn find_edge(configured: Option<&str>, env: &dyn Fn(&str) -> Option<String>) -> Option<PathBuf>`; `pub fn find_program(program: &str) -> Option<PathBuf>`; `pub fn profile_in_use(profile: &Path) -> bool`; `pub fn move_downloads(from: &Path, to: &Path) -> Vec<String>`.
  - Tests: `common::fake_browser::{exe, FakeEdges}` with `FakeEdges::new(root)`, `profile`, `starts`, `port`, `running`, `first_pages`, `set_pages`, `quit`, `gone_within`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the fake Edge, its helper and the failing tests**

**Create `crates/dots-core/src/bin/fake-browser.rs`:**

```rust
//! Test stand-in for Microsoft Edge in dots-core tests: `fake-browser --user-data-dir=<dir>
//! --remote-debugging-port=<port> [other switches] [address]`.
//!
//! - It holds `<dir>/lockfile` as Edge does: while it runs, no other process can open that
//!   file for writing. A start on a profile another process holds exits 0 at once without
//!   serving, as Edge hands over to the Edge that holds it.
//! - It answers enough of Edge's debugging HTTP on 127.0.0.1:<port>: `GET /json/version`;
//!   `GET /json/list` (its pages, most recent first, then a `service_worker` target, as
//!   Edge lists an extension's); `PUT /json/close/<id>`, which closes a page (the process
//!   exits once none is left).
//! - Test controls: `POST /fake/pages` with a JSON array of addresses makes those its pages,
//!   most recent first; `POST /fake/quit` exits at once, as when the person closes the window.
//! - Its first pages are `<dir>/fake-pages.json` (a JSON array) when that file exists, else the
//!   address it was given, else `about:blank`.
//! - Each start appends `{"args": [...], "port": <port>, "pid": <pid>}` to
//!   `<dir>/fake-browser.jsonl`. It exits when `<dir>` is gone, and after 120 s.

use std::fs::OpenOptions;
use std::io::Write as _;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use axum::extract::{Path as UrlPath, State};
use axum::http::StatusCode;
use axum::routing::{get, post, put};
use axum::{Json, Router};
use serde_json::{json, Value};

struct Fake {
    port: u16,
    next: AtomicU64,
    /// `(id, address)`, most recent first.
    pages: Mutex<Vec<(String, String)>>,
}

impl Fake {
    fn page(&self, url: String) -> (String, String) {
        (format!("P{}", self.next.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)), url)
    }
}

/// The value of `--name=value`.
fn switch(args: &[String], name: &str) -> Option<String> {
    args.iter()
        .find_map(|a| a.strip_prefix(name)?.strip_prefix('=').map(str::to_string))
}

/// Holds `<dir>/lockfile` as Edge does; `None` when another process holds it.
fn hold_lock(dir: &Path) -> Option<std::fs::File> {
    let mut options = OpenOptions::new();
    options.write(true).create(true);
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        use std::os::windows::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
        // FILE_SHARE_READ: others may read the file, not open it for writing.
        options.share_mode(1);
    }
    let file = options.open(dir.join("lockfile")).ok()?;
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    file.try_lock().ok()?;
    Some(file)
}

fn exit_soon() {
    tokio::spawn(async {
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100)).await;
        std::process::exit(0);
    });
}

async fn version(State(fake): State<Arc<Fake>>) -> Json<Value> {
    Json(json!({
        "Browser": "Edg/0.0.0.0 (fake)",
        "Protocol-Version": "1.3",
        "webSocketDebuggerUrl": format!("ws://127.0.0.1:{}/devtools/browser/fake", fake.port)
    }))
}

async fn list(State(fake): State<Arc<Fake>>) -> Json<Value> {
    let mut targets: Vec<Value> = fake
        .pages
        .lock()
        .unwrap()
        .iter()
        .map(|(id, url)| {
            json!({
                "description": "", "id": id, "title": "", "type": "page", "url": url,
                "webSocketDebuggerUrl": format!("ws://127.0.0.1:{}/devtools/page/{id}", fake.port)
            })
        })
        .collect();
    targets.push(json!({
        "description": "", "id": "SW", "title": "Service Worker", "type": "service_worker",
        "url": "chrome-extension://fake/background.js"
    }));
    Json(Value::Array(targets))
}

async fn close(
    State(fake): State<Arc<Fake>>,
    UrlPath(id): UrlPath<String>,
) -> (StatusCode, String) {
    let mut pages = fake.pages.lock().unwrap();
    let Some(i) = pages.iter().position(|(page, _)| *page == id) else {
        return (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, format!("No such target id: {id}"));
    };
    pages.remove(i);
    if pages.is_empty() {
        exit_soon();
    }
    (StatusCode::OK, "Target is closing".to_string())
}

async fn set_pages(State(fake): State<Arc<Fake>>, Json(urls): Json<Vec<String>>) -> StatusCode {
    let pages: Vec<(String, String)> = urls.into_iter().map(|url| fake.page(url)).collect();
    *fake.pages.lock().unwrap() = pages;
    StatusCode::OK
}

async fn quit() -> StatusCode {
    exit_soon();
    StatusCode::OK
}

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    let dir = PathBuf::from(switch(&args, "--user-data-dir").expect("--user-data-dir"));
    let port: u16 = switch(&args, "--remote-debugging-port")
        .expect("--remote-debugging-port")
        .parse()
        .expect("a port");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
    // Edge hands over to the Edge that holds the profile, and exits.
    let Some(_lock) = hold_lock(&dir) else {
        return;
    };
    let Ok(listener) = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(("127.0.0.1", port)).await else {
        std::process::exit(2);
    };
    let mut log = OpenOptions::new()
        .create(true)
        .append(true)
        .open(dir.join("fake-browser.jsonl"))
        .unwrap();
    writeln!(
        log,
        "{}",
        json!({"args": args, "port": port, "pid": std::process::id()})
    )
    .unwrap();
    let first: Vec<String> = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("fake-pages.json"))
        .ok()
        .and_then(|text| serde_json::from_str(&text).ok())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| {
            vec![args
                .iter()
                .rev()
                .find(|a| !a.starts_with("--"))
                .cloned()
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "about:blank".to_string())]
        });
    let fake = Arc::new(Fake {
        port,
        next: AtomicU64::new(0),
        pages: Mutex::new(Vec::new()),
    });
    let pages: Vec<(String, String)> = first.into_iter().map(|url| fake.page(url)).collect();
    *fake.pages.lock().unwrap() = pages;
    // Gone with its test: when the profile's folder is removed, or after 120 s.
    let watched = dir.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let end = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(120);
        while Instant::now() < end && watched.exists() {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
        }
        std::process::exit(0);
    });
    let app = Router::new()
        .route("/json/version", get(version))
        .route("/json/list", get(list))
        .route("/json/close/{id}", put(close))
        .route("/fake/pages", post(set_pages))
        .route("/fake/quit", post(quit))
        .with_state(fake);
    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
}
```

**Create `crates/dots-core/tests/common/fake_browser.rs`:**

```rust
//! The test stand-in for Microsoft Edge, `fake-browser` (`src/bin/fake-browser.rs`): its path,
//! for `Settings.edge_path` or `EdgeHost::open`; what each start on a dot's profile recorded;
//! and its controls. Every fake running on a profile under `root` is told to quit when this is
//! dropped, so a failed test leaves no Edge behind.

use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::net::TcpStream;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use serde_json::Value;

/// The program `fake-browser`.
pub fn exe() -> String {
    env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_fake-browser").to_string()
}

/// The fakes on the profiles under `root` (`<data_dir>/browser`).
pub struct FakeEdges {
    pub root: PathBuf,
}

impl FakeEdges {
    pub fn new(root: impl Into<PathBuf>) -> Self {
        Self { root: root.into() }
    }

    pub fn profile(&self, dot_id: &str) -> PathBuf {
        self.root.join(dot_id).join("profile")
    }

    /// Every start on the dot's profile, oldest first: `{"args": [...], "port": n, "pid": n}`.
    pub fn starts(&self, dot_id: &str) -> Vec<Value> {
        match std::fs::read_to_string(self.profile(dot_id).join("fake-browser.jsonl")) {
            Ok(text) => text
                .lines()
                .map(|line| serde_json::from_str(line).unwrap())
                .collect(),
            Err(_) => Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    /// The port of the newest start.
    pub fn port(&self, dot_id: &str) -> Option<u16> {
        self.starts(dot_id)
            .last()
            .and_then(|start| start["port"].as_u64())
            .map(|port| port as u16)
    }

    /// Whether a fake holds the dot's profile.
    pub fn running(&self, dot_id: &str) -> bool {
        dots_core::browser::host::profile_in_use(&self.profile(dot_id))
    }

    /// The pages the next start opens with, most recent first.
    pub fn first_pages(&self, dot_id: &str, urls: &[&str]) {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(self.profile(dot_id)).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(
            self.profile(dot_id).join("fake-pages.json"),
            serde_json::to_string(urls).unwrap(),
        )
        .unwrap();
    }

    /// Makes `urls` the pages of the running fake, most recent first.
    pub fn set_pages(&self, dot_id: &str, urls: &[&str]) {
        let port = self.port(dot_id).expect("a started fake");
        assert!(
            post(port, "/fake/pages", &serde_json::to_string(urls).unwrap()),
            "the fake did not take its pages"
        );
    }

    /// Ends the running fake at once, as when the person closes the window.
    pub fn quit(&self, dot_id: &str) {
        if let Some(port) = self.port(dot_id) {
            post(port, "/fake/quit", "");
        }
    }

    /// Whether no fake holds the dot's profile within `limit`.
    pub async fn gone_within(&self, dot_id: &str, limit: Duration) -> bool {
        let deadline = Instant::now() + limit;
        while self.running(dot_id) {
            if Instant::now() > deadline {
                return false;
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)).await;
        }
        true
    }
}

impl Drop for FakeEdges {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let Ok(dots) = std::fs::read_dir(&self.root) else {
            return;
        };
        for dot in dots.flatten() {
            let id = dot.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
            if self.running(&id) {
                self.quit(&id);
            }
        }
        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
        while Instant::now() < deadline
            && std::fs::read_dir(&self.root)
                .map(|dots| {
                    dots.flatten()
                        .any(|d| self.running(&d.file_name().to_string_lossy()))
                })
                .unwrap_or(false)
        {
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
        }
    }
}

/// A plain HTTP POST to the fake on `port`. True when it answered 200.
fn post(port: u16, path: &str, body: &str) -> bool {
    let Ok(mut stream) = TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)) else {
        return false;
    };
    let _ = stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2)));
    let request = format!(
        "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\n\
         Content-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n{body}",
        body.len()
    );
    if stream.write_all(request.as_bytes()).is_err() {
        return false;
    }
    let mut answer = String::new();
    let _ = stream.read_to_string(&mut answer);
    answer.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 200")
}
```

**Create `crates/dots-core/tests/browser_host_test.rs`:**

```rust
//! Microsoft Edge for dots (browser spec section 2.1) through `EdgeHost`, with `fake-browser`
//! in Edge's place: the switches and profile of a start, a second open that finds it (also
//! from a Dots that started again), the pages it lists, closing it, a window the person
//! closed, a profile another Edge holds, an Edge that is not there, and moving downloads.

mod common;

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::time::Duration;

use common::fake_browser::{exe, FakeEdges};
use common::playwright::JSON_LIST;
use dots_core::browser::host::{
    edge_args, edge_candidates, find_edge, move_downloads, page_urls, BrowserHost, EdgeHost,
    HostError, Opened, EDGE_FLAGS,
};
use serde_json::Value;

/// Dropped in this order: the fakes quit, then the folder goes.
struct Env {
    edges: FakeEdges,
    host: EdgeHost,
    root: PathBuf,
    dir: tempfile::TempDir,
}

fn env() -> Env {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let root = dir.path().join("browser");
    Env {
        edges: FakeEdges::new(&root),
        host: EdgeHost::new(&root),
        root,
        dir,
    }
}

fn edge() -> PathBuf {
    PathBuf::from(exe())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn edge_starts_on_the_dots_profile_with_its_switches() {
    let e = env();
    let opened = e.host.open("D1", &edge()).await.unwrap();
    assert!(opened.started);
    let starts = e.edges.starts("D1");
    assert_eq!(starts.len(), 1);
    let args: Vec<String> = serde_json::from_value(starts[0]["args"].clone()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(args, edge_args(&e.host.profile("D1"), opened.port));
    assert_eq!(
        args[0],
        format!("--user-data-dir={}", e.root.join("D1").join("profile").display())
    );
    assert_eq!(args[1], "--remote-debugging-address=127.0.0.1");
    assert_eq!(args[2], format!("--remote-debugging-port={}", opened.port));
    assert_eq!(args[3..10], EDGE_FLAGS);
    assert_eq!(args[10], "about:blank");
    // Downloads go to the dot's own folder, without a question.
    let prefs: Value = serde_json::from_str(
        &std::fs::read_to_string(e.host.profile("D1").join("Default").join("Preferences"))
            .unwrap(),
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        prefs["download"]["default_directory"].as_str(),
        Some(e.host.downloads("D1").to_str().unwrap())
    );
    assert_eq!(prefs["download"]["prompt_for_download"], false);
    assert!(e.host.downloads("D1").is_dir());
    // Its pages: the blank page; the extension's service worker is not one.
    assert_eq!(e.host.pages(opened.port).await.unwrap(), ["about:blank"]);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_second_open_finds_the_running_edge() {
    let e = env();
    let first = e.host.open("D1", &edge()).await.unwrap();
    let again = e.host.open("D1", &edge()).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        again,
        Opened {
            port: first.port,
            started: false
        }
    );
    assert_eq!(e.edges.starts("D1").len(), 1);
    // A Dots that starts again finds it through the port it kept.
    let restarted = EdgeHost::new(&e.root);
    assert_eq!(
        restarted.open("D1", &edge()).await.unwrap(),
        Opened {
            port: first.port,
            started: false
        }
    );
    // Another dot gets an Edge of its own.
    let other = e.host.open("D2", &edge()).await.unwrap();
    assert_ne!(other.port, first.port);
    assert!(e.edges.running("D1") && e.edges.running("D2"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn closing_ends_it_and_a_closed_window_starts_again() {
    let e = env();
    e.host.open("D1", &edge()).await.unwrap();
    e.host.close("D1").await;
    assert!(e.edges.gone_within("D1", Duration::from_secs(10)).await);
    assert!(e.host.open("D1", &edge()).await.unwrap().started);
    // The person closes the window: the next open starts Edge again on the same profile.
    e.edges.quit("D1");
    assert!(e.edges.gone_within("D1", Duration::from_secs(10)).await);
    assert!(e.host.open("D1", &edge()).await.unwrap().started);
    assert_eq!(e.edges.starts("D1").len(), 3);
    // Closing an Edge that does not run does nothing.
    e.host.close("D9").await;
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn its_pages_are_the_page_targets_of_its_list() {
    let targets: Vec<Value> = serde_json::from_str(JSON_LIST).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        page_urls(&targets),
        ["edge://sync-confirmation-dialog/", "http://127.0.0.1:54428/cart"]
    );
    let e = env();
    let opened = e.host.open("D1", &edge()).await.unwrap();
    e.edges
        .set_pages("D1", &["https://shop.com/other", "https://shop.com/cart"]);
    assert_eq!(
        e.host.pages(opened.port).await.unwrap(),
        ["https://shop.com/other", "https://shop.com/cart"]
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_profile_held_by_another_edge_is_refused() {
    let e = env();
    // An Edge started outside Dots on the dot's profile: the fake, by hand.
    let port = std::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0")
        .unwrap()
        .local_addr()
        .unwrap()
        .port();
    let mut outside = tokio::process::Command::new(exe());
    outside
        .arg(format!("--user-data-dir={}", e.host.profile("D1").display()))
        .arg(format!("--remote-debugging-port={port}"))
        .kill_on_drop(true);
    dots_core::proc::hide_window(&mut outside);
    let _outside = outside.spawn().unwrap();
    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(10);
    while !e.edges.running("D1") {
        assert!(std::time::Instant::now() < deadline, "the outside fake did not start");
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)).await;
    }
    assert_eq!(
        e.host.open("D1", &edge()).await,
        Err(HostError::ProfileInUse)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        HostError::ProfileInUse.to_string(),
        "This dot's browser profile is in use by another Edge window; close it and try again."
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn an_edge_that_is_not_there_is_not_found() {
    let e = env();
    let missing = e.dir.path().join("no-edge.exe");
    assert_eq!(
        e.host.open("D1", &missing).await,
        Err(HostError::NotFound)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        HostError::NotFound.to_string(),
        "Microsoft Edge was not found; browser use needs it (Settings → Edge path)."
    );
    let installed = |key: &str| match key {
        "ProgramFiles(x86)" => Some("C:\\PF86".to_string()),
        "ProgramFiles" => Some("C:\\PF".to_string()),
        _ => None,
    };
    let under = |base: &str| {
        Path::new(base)
            .join("Microsoft")
            .join("Edge")
            .join("Application")
            .join("msedge.exe")
    };
    assert_eq!(edge_candidates(None, &installed), [under("C:\\PF86"), under("C:\\PF")]);
    assert_eq!(edge_candidates(Some("  "), &installed).len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(
        edge_candidates(Some(" D:/Edge/msedge.exe "), &installed),
        [PathBuf::from("D:/Edge/msedge.exe")]
    );
    assert_eq!(find_edge(Some(exe().as_str()), &installed), Some(edge()));
    // A path that is set but missing is not found: no fall back to an installed Edge.
    assert_eq!(find_edge(Some(missing.to_str().unwrap()), &installed), None);
}

#[test]
fn finished_downloads_move_into_the_workspace() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let from = dir.path().join("downloads");
    let to = dir.path().join("ws").join("downloads");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&from).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(from.join("statement.pdf"), "new").unwrap();
    std::fs::write(from.join("report.csv"), "b").unwrap();
    std::fs::write(from.join("big.zip.crdownload"), "c").unwrap();
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&to).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(to.join("statement.pdf"), "old").unwrap();

    assert_eq!(move_downloads(&from, &to), ["report.csv", "statement (2).pdf"]);
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(to.join("statement (2).pdf")).unwrap(), "new");
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(to.join("statement.pdf")).unwrap(), "old");
    assert!(from.join("big.zip.crdownload").exists(), "still being written");
    assert!(move_downloads(&from, &to).is_empty());
    assert!(move_downloads(&dir.path().join("none"), &to).is_empty());
}
```
