**Create `crates/dots-core/tests/claude_browser_test.rs`:**

```rust
//! Browser use on the Claude engine (browser spec sections 2.2 and 3.6), driven through
//! `fake-claude`, with `fake-browser` as Edge and `fake-mcp` as Playwright: the run's
//! `--mcp-config` and `--allowedTools`, its calls through the gateway route `/mcp/s/browser`,
//! a late approval that is skipped and told while its rule is saved, and a Library server
//! named `browser`.

mod common;

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use common::fake_browser::{cart_script, exe, playwright_command, FakeEdges, CART};
use common::fake_mcp::{tool, FakeMcp};
use common::{spec, temp_store, wait_status};
use dots_core::approvals::{grant_key, ApprovalHub, DecideEffect};
use dots_core::browser::host::EdgeHost;
use dots_core::browser::{Browsers, LATE_APPROVAL};
use dots_core::engine::claude::ClaudeEngine;
use dots_core::engine::Engine;
use dots_core::events::new_bus;
use dots_core::library::LibraryConfig;
use dots_core::library_service::LibraryService;
use dots_core::model::{
    ApplyState, ApprovalStatus, Dot, DotSpec, EngineKind, NewRun, Run, RunEventRecord,
    RunStatus, TriggerKind, WorkspaceMode,
};
use dots_core::policy::{Action, Rule};
use dots_core::runner::{Runner, RunnerConfig};
use dots_core::server::{bind, serve, ServerState};
use dots_core::settings::Settings;
use dots_core::store::Store;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use tempfile::TempDir;
use tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken;

/// Dropped in this order: the fakes quit, then the rest, then the folder.
struct Env {
    edges: FakeEdges,
    playwright: FakeMcp,
    store: Store,
    hub: Arc<ApprovalHub>,
    runner: Arc<Runner>,
    lib: LibraryService,
    base: String,
    args_out: PathBuf,
    dir: TempDir,
}

/// A runner with the Claude engine on `fake-claude` playing `script`, the local server, and
/// the dots' browsers on `fake-browser` and `fake-mcp`.
async fn env(script: Value) -> Env {
    let (dir, store) = temp_store().await;
    let bus = new_bus();
    let hub = Arc::new(ApprovalHub::new(store.clone(), bus.clone()));
    let script_path = dir.path().join("script.json");
    std::fs::write(&script_path, script.to_string()).unwrap();
    let args_out = dir.path().join("args.json");
    let engine = ClaudeEngine::new(
        PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_fake-claude")),
        dir.path().join("scratch"),
    )
    .with_env("FAKE_CLAUDE_SCRIPT", &script_path.to_string_lossy())
    .with_env("FAKE_CLAUDE_ARGS_OUT", &args_out.to_string_lossy());
    let listener = bind(0).await.unwrap();
    let base = format!("http://127.0.0.1:{}", listener.local_addr().unwrap().port());
    let mut engines: HashMap<EngineKind, Arc<dyn Engine>> = HashMap::new();
    engines.insert(EngineKind::Claude, Arc::new(engine));
    let runner = Runner::new(
        store.clone(),
        bus.clone(),
        engines,
        RunnerConfig {
            max_concurrent: 2,
            mcp_url: format!("{base}/mcp"),
            worktrees_dir: dir.path().join("wt"),
            cancel_grace: Duration::from_secs(2),
            plugins_dir: dir.path().join("plugins"),
        },
    );
    store
        .put_settings(&Settings {
            edge_path: Some(exe()),
            ..Settings::default()
        })
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let root = dir.path().join("browser");
    let playwright = FakeMcp::new(cart_script());
    runner.set_browsers(Browsers::new(
        store.clone(),
        Arc::new(EdgeHost::new(&root)),
        playwright_command(&playwright),
    ));
    serve(
        listener,
        ServerState {
            store: store.clone(),
            runner: runner.clone(),
            hub: hub.clone(),
        },
        CancellationToken::new(),
    );
    runner.spawn_dispatcher(CancellationToken::new());
    Env {
        edges: FakeEdges::new(&root),
        playwright,
        lib: LibraryService::new(store.clone(), bus),
        store,
        hub,
        runner,
        base,
        args_out,
        dir,
    }
}

/// A Claude dot with the browser on, in the test folder; asking parks at once. Its Edge opens
/// on the cart page.
async fn browser_dot(e: &Env, edit: impl FnOnce(&mut DotSpec)) -> Dot {
    let mut s = spec("shopper");
    s.workdir = e.dir.path().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
    s.workspace_mode = WorkspaceMode::Folder;
    s.engine = EngineKind::Claude;
    s.browser = true;
    s.approval_wait_secs = 0;
    edit(&mut s);
    let dot = e.store.create_dot(&s).await.unwrap();
    e.edges.first_pages(&dot.id, &[CART]);
    dot
}

async fn start(e: &Env, dot: &Dot) -> Run {
    e.runner
        .enqueue(NewRun::new(&dot.id, TriggerKind::Manual))
        .await
        .unwrap()
}

/// What `fake-claude` recorded of its last start: `args`, `mcp_config`, `prompt`, ...
fn recorded(e: &Env) -> Value {
    serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(&e.args_out).unwrap()).unwrap()
}

/// The values after `flag`, up to the next flag.
fn values_of(args: &[String], flag: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    args.iter()
        .skip_while(|a| *a != flag)
        .skip(1)
        .take_while(|a| !a.starts_with("--"))
        .cloned()
        .collect()
}

async fn events(e: &Env, run_id: &str, kind: &str) -> Vec<RunEventRecord> {
    e.store
        .list_events(run_id, 0)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|ev| ev.kind == kind)
        .collect()
}

fn init() -> Value {
    json!({"emit": {"type": "system", "subtype": "init", "session_id": "$SESSION"}})
}

fn success() -> Value {
    json!({"emit": {"type": "result", "subtype": "success", "is_error": false, "result": "done",
                    "usage": {"input_tokens": 1, "output_tokens": 1}}})
}

fn browser_call(tool: &str, arguments: Value) -> Value {
    json!({"mcp_call": {"server": "browser", "tool": tool, "arguments": arguments}})
}

/// The resume run of `parent`, once the runner made it.
async fn resume_child(e: &Env, parent: &Run) -> Run {
    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(10);
    loop {
        let runs = e.store.list_runs(Some(&parent.dot_id), 10).await.unwrap();
        if let Some(r) = runs
            .into_iter()
            .find(|r| r.parent_run_id.as_deref() == Some(parent.id.as_str()))
        {
            return r;
        }
        assert!(std::time::Instant::now() < deadline, "no resume run appeared");
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
    }
}

fn called(e: &Env) -> Vec<String> {
    e.playwright
        .received("tools/call")
        .iter()
        .map(|m| m["params"]["name"].as_str().unwrap().to_string())
        .collect()
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_claude_run_reaches_the_browser_through_the_gateway() {
    let e = env(json!([
        init(),
        {"mcp_list": "browser"},
        browser_call("browser_snapshot", json!({})),
        browser_call("browser_click", json!({"target": "e6"})),
        success()
    ]))
    .await;
    let dot = browser_dot(&e, |s| {
        s.policy
            .rules
            .push(Rule::new("Browser(shop.com)", Action::Allow))
    })
    .await;
    let run = start(&e, &dot).await;
    wait_status(&e.store, &run.id, RunStatus::Succeeded, 30).await;

    let rec = recorded(&e);
    let args: Vec<String> = serde_json::from_value(rec["args"].clone()).unwrap();
    let allowed = values_of(&args, "--allowedTools");
    assert!(allowed.contains(&"mcp__browser__*".to_string()), "{args:?}");
    assert!(
        values_of(&args, "--disallowedTools")
            .iter()
            .all(|t| !t.starts_with("Browser")),
        "{args:?}"
    );
    let servers = &rec["mcp_config"]["mcpServers"];
    assert_eq!(servers.as_object().unwrap().len(), 2, "{servers}");
    assert_eq!(
        servers["browser"],
        json!({"type": "http", "url": format!("{}/mcp/s/browser", e.base),
               "headers": {"Authorization": servers["dots"]["headers"]["Authorization"]}})
    );
    // The CLI saw the pinned list, and its calls went through the gate to Playwright.
    let listed = events(&e, &run.id, "raw").await;
    let listed: Value = serde_json::from_str(listed[0].data["line"].as_str().unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(listed["tools"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 25);
    assert_eq!(listed["tools"][0], "browser_close");
    let results = events(&e, &run.id, "tool_result").await;
    let clicked = results[1].data["output"].as_str().unwrap();
    assert!(clicked.starts_with("Dots: Clicked \"Home\"\n"), "{clicked}");
    assert_eq!(called(&e), ["browser_snapshot", "browser_click"]);
    // The run's end closed its Playwright server and its Edge.
    assert!(e.playwright.gone_within(Duration::from_secs(10)).await);
    assert!(e.edges.gone_within(&dot.id, Duration::from_secs(10)).await);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_late_browser_approval_is_skipped_and_the_dot_is_told() {
    let e = env(json!([
        init(),
        browser_call("browser_snapshot", json!({})),
        browser_call("browser_click", json!({"target": "e16"})),
        success()
    ]))
    .await;
    let dot = browser_dot(&e, |_| {}).await;
    let parent = start(&e, &dot).await;
    wait_status(&e.store, &parent.id, RunStatus::AwaitingApproval, 30).await;
    let approval = e
        .store
        .approvals_for_run(&parent.id)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .remove(0);
    assert_eq!(approval.tool, "mcp__browser__browser_click");
    // Approved after the run parked, with "always allow on shop.com".
    let decided = e
        .hub
        .decide_with_rule(
            &approval.id,
            true,
            None,
            Some(Rule::new("Browser(shop.com)", Action::Allow)),
        )
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(decided, DecideEffect::Parked(_)));
    e.runner.maybe_resume(&parent.id).await.unwrap();
    let child = resume_child(&e, &parent).await;
    let message = child.payload.as_ref().unwrap()["message"]
        .as_str()
        .unwrap()
        .to_string();
    assert!(
        message.contains(&format!(
            "- Approval #{} for a browser action: {LATE_APPROVAL}",
            approval.id
        )),
        "{message}"
    );
    assert!(!message.contains("granted"), "{message}");
    let skipped = e.store.get_approval(&approval.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(skipped.status, ApprovalStatus::Approved);
    assert_eq!(skipped.apply_state, Some(ApplyState::Skipped));
    assert!(skipped.resolved);
    assert_eq!(
        e.store
            .take_mcp_grant(
                &parent.root_run_id,
                &approval.tool,
                &grant_key(&approval.tool, &approval.input)
            )
            .await
            .unwrap(),
        None,
        "no grant: the click is never retried by itself"
    );
    // The rule chosen with it is saved.
    assert!(e
        .store
        .get_dot(&dot.id)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .spec
        .policy
        .rules
        .contains(&Rule::new("Browser(shop.com)", Action::Allow)));
    // The resumed run is told; its own click of "Pay now" is a new call, which asks again.
    wait_status(&e.store, &child.id, RunStatus::AwaitingApproval, 30).await;
    assert_eq!(recorded(&e)["prompt"].as_str().unwrap(), message);
    assert!(!called(&e).contains(&"browser_click".to_string()));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_library_server_named_browser_leaves_the_built_in_out() {
    let e = env(json!([init(), success()])).await;
    let fake = FakeMcp::new(json!({"tools": [tool("look", true)]}));
    let probe = e.lib.probe(&fake.stdio()).await.unwrap();
    e.lib
        .add(
            "browser",
            LibraryConfig::McpServer(fake.stdio()),
            Some(&probe.fingerprint),
        )
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let dot = browser_dot(&e, |s| s.uses.mcp_servers = vec!["browser".into()]).await;
    let run = start(&e, &dot).await;
    wait_status(&e.store, &run.id, RunStatus::Succeeded, 30).await;
    let notices: Vec<String> = events(&e, &run.id, "notice")
        .await
        .iter()
        .map(|ev| ev.data["text"].as_str().unwrap().to_string())
        .collect();
    assert!(
        notices.contains(
            &"browser: an MCP server this dot uses is named browser, so the built-in browser \
              is left out of this run; rename that server in the Library to use both"
                .to_string()
        ),
        "{notices:?}"
    );
    assert!(e.edges.starts(&dot.id).is_empty());
}
```

**Create `crates/dots-core/tests/local_browser_test.rs`:**

```rust
//! Browser use on the local engine (browser spec sections 2.2, 3.5 and 3.6), with a scripted
//! endpoint, `fake-browser` as Edge and `fake-mcp` as Playwright: the 25 functions offered to a
//! dot with the browser and none to one without, calls decided by the gate, password typing
//! refused before Playwright, and a late approval that Dots does not perform.

mod common;
mod llm;

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use common::fake_browser::{cart_script, exe, playwright_command, FakeEdges, CART};
use common::fake_mcp::FakeMcp;
use common::{spec, temp_store, wait_status};
use dots_core::approvals::{ApprovalHub, DecideEffect};
use dots_core::browser::gate::PASSWORD_REFUSAL;
use dots_core::browser::host::EdgeHost;
use dots_core::browser::{Browsers, LATE_APPROVAL};
use dots_core::engine::local::LocalEngine;
use dots_core::engine::Engine;
use dots_core::events::new_bus;
use dots_core::model::{ApplyState, Dot, EngineKind, NewRun, Run, RunStatus, TriggerKind, WorkspaceMode};
use dots_core::policy::{Action, Rule};
use dots_core::runner::{Runner, RunnerConfig};
use dots_core::settings::Settings;
use dots_core::store::Store;
use llm::{call, messages, text, tool_result, Llm};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use tempfile::TempDir;
use tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken;

/// Dropped in this order: the fakes quit, then the rest, then the folder.
struct Env {
    edges: FakeEdges,
    playwright: FakeMcp,
    store: Store,
    hub: Arc<ApprovalHub>,
    runner: Arc<Runner>,
    ws: PathBuf,
    _dir: TempDir,
}

async fn env() -> Env {
    let (dir, store) = temp_store().await;
    let ws = dir.path().join("ws");
    std::fs::create_dir(&ws).unwrap();
    let bus = new_bus();
    let hub = Arc::new(ApprovalHub::new(store.clone(), bus.clone()));
    let engine =
        LocalEngine::with_retry_delays(store.clone(), hub.clone(), vec![Duration::ZERO; 2]);
    hub.set_self_apply([EngineKind::Local]);
    let mut engines: HashMap<EngineKind, Arc<dyn Engine>> = HashMap::new();
    engines.insert(EngineKind::Local, Arc::new(engine));
    let runner = Runner::new(
        store.clone(),
        bus,
        engines,
        RunnerConfig {
            max_concurrent: 2,
            mcp_url: "http://127.0.0.1:1/mcp".into(),
            worktrees_dir: dir.path().join("wt"),
            cancel_grace: Duration::from_millis(500),
            plugins_dir: dir.path().join("plugins"),
        },
    );
    store
        .put_settings(&Settings {
            edge_path: Some(exe()),
            ..Settings::default()
        })
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let root = dir.path().join("browser");
    let playwright = FakeMcp::new(cart_script());
    runner.set_browsers(Browsers::new(
        store.clone(),
        Arc::new(EdgeHost::new(&root)),
        playwright_command(&playwright),
    ));
    runner.spawn_dispatcher(CancellationToken::new());
    Env {
        edges: FakeEdges::new(&root),
        playwright,
        store,
        hub,
        runner,
        ws,
        _dir: dir,
    }
}

/// A local dot on `llm`, with the browser on or off, under `rules`; asking parks at once. Its
/// Edge opens on the cart page.
async fn local_dot(e: &Env, llm: &Llm, browser: bool, rules: &[(&str, Action)]) -> Dot {
    let mut s = spec("loco");
    s.workdir = e.ws.to_string_lossy().into_owned();
    s.workspace_mode = WorkspaceMode::Folder;
    s.engine = EngineKind::Local;
    s.endpoint_url = Some(llm.endpoint());
    s.model = "test-model".into();
    s.approval_wait_secs = 0;
    s.browser = browser;
    s.policy
        .rules
        .extend(rules.iter().map(|(tool, action)| Rule::new(tool, *action)));
    let dot = e.store.create_dot(&s).await.unwrap();
    e.edges.first_pages(&dot.id, &[CART]);
    dot
}

async fn run(e: &Env, dot: &Dot) -> Run {
    e.runner
        .enqueue(NewRun::new(&dot.id, TriggerKind::Manual))
        .await
        .unwrap()
}

/// The function names of a recorded request.
fn functions(req: &Value) -> Vec<String> {
    req["tools"]
        .as_array()
        .unwrap()
        .iter()
        .map(|t| t["function"]["name"].as_str().unwrap().to_string())
        .collect()
}

fn called(e: &Env) -> Vec<String> {
    e.playwright
        .received("tools/call")
        .iter()
        .map(|m| m["params"]["name"].as_str().unwrap().to_string())
        .collect()
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_local_engine_offers_the_browser_and_its_gate_decides() {
    let e = env().await;
    let llm = Llm::start(vec![
        call("c1", "mcp__browser__browser_snapshot", json!({})),
        call("c2", "mcp__browser__browser_click", json!({"target": "e6"})),
        text("done"),
    ])
    .await;
    let dot = local_dot(&e, &llm, true, &[("Browser(shop.com)", Action::Allow)]).await;
    let r = run(&e, &dot).await;
    wait_status(&e.store, &r.id, RunStatus::Succeeded, 30).await;
    let reqs = llm.requests();
    let names = functions(&reqs[0]);
    assert_eq!(
        names.iter().filter(|n| n.starts_with("mcp__browser__")).count(),
        25
    );
    assert!(names.contains(&"mcp__browser__browser_click".to_string()));
    let clicked = tool_result(&reqs[2], "c2");
    assert!(clicked.starts_with("Dots: Clicked \"Home\"\n"), "{clicked}");
    assert_eq!(called(&e), ["browser_snapshot", "browser_click"]);
    assert!(e.edges.gone_within(&dot.id, Duration::from_secs(10)).await);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_dot_without_the_browser_has_no_browser_tools() {
    let e = env().await;
    let llm = Llm::start(vec![text("done")]).await;
    let dot = local_dot(&e, &llm, false, &[]).await;
    let r = run(&e, &dot).await;
    wait_status(&e.store, &r.id, RunStatus::Succeeded, 30).await;
    assert!(functions(&llm.requests()[0])
        .iter()
        .all(|n| !n.starts_with("mcp__browser__")));
    assert!(e.edges.starts(&dot.id).is_empty());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn password_typing_is_refused_before_playwright() {
    let e = env().await;
    let llm = Llm::start(vec![
        call("c1", "mcp__browser__browser_snapshot", json!({})),
        call(
            "c2",
            "mcp__browser__browser_type",
            json!({"target": "e13", "text": "hunter2"}),
        ),
        text("done"),
    ])
    .await;
    let everything = [
        ("Browser(*)", Action::Allow),
        ("BrowserRisky(*)", Action::Allow),
    ];
    let dot = local_dot(&e, &llm, true, &everything).await;
    let r = run(&e, &dot).await;
    wait_status(&e.store, &r.id, RunStatus::Succeeded, 30).await;
    assert_eq!(tool_result(&llm.requests()[2], "c2"), PASSWORD_REFUSAL);
    assert_eq!(called(&e), ["browser_snapshot"]);
    assert!(e.store.approvals_for_run(&r.id).await.unwrap().is_empty());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_late_browser_approval_is_not_performed_by_dots() {
    let e = env().await;
    let llm = Llm::start(vec![
        call("c1", "mcp__browser__browser_snapshot", json!({})),
        call("c2", "mcp__browser__browser_click", json!({"target": "e16"})),
        text("Waiting for the approval."),
        text("I will take a snapshot first."),
    ])
    .await;
    let dot = local_dot(&e, &llm, true, &[("Browser(shop.com)", Action::Allow)]).await;
    let parent = run(&e, &dot).await;
    wait_status(&e.store, &parent.id, RunStatus::AwaitingApproval, 30).await;
    let approval = e
        .store
        .approvals_for_run(&parent.id)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .remove(0);
    assert!(approval.self_apply, "a local dot's late approvals are Dots' to perform");
    let decided = e.hub.decide(&approval.id, true, None).await.unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(decided, DecideEffect::Parked(_)));
    e.runner.maybe_resume(&parent.id).await.unwrap();
    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(10);
    let child = loop {
        let runs = e.store.list_runs(Some(&dot.id), 10).await.unwrap();
        if let Some(r) = runs
            .into_iter()
            .find(|r| r.parent_run_id.as_deref() == Some(parent.id.as_str()))
        {
            break r;
        }
        assert!(std::time::Instant::now() < deadline, "no resume run appeared");
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
    };
    wait_status(&e.store, &child.id, RunStatus::Succeeded, 30).await;
    let skipped = e.store.get_approval(&approval.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(skipped.apply_state, Some(ApplyState::Skipped));
    let last = llm.requests().pop().unwrap();
    assert!(
        messages(&last).iter().any(|m| m["role"] == "user"
            && m["content"].as_str().is_some_and(|c| c.contains(LATE_APPROVAL))),
        "{last:#}"
    );
    assert_eq!(called(&e), ["browser_snapshot"]);
}
```

**Create `crates/dots-core/src/store/browser.rs`:**

```rust
//! The store's part of browser use (browser spec section 3.6).

use super::Store;
use crate::Result;

impl Store {
    /// Records that an approved browser call is not performed: it was approved after its run
    /// parked, and the page may have moved on. Its `apply_state` becomes `skipped`. False when
    /// the call is not approved, or already has another outcome.
    pub async fn skip_late_browser_call(&self, approval_id: &str) -> Result<bool> {
        let res = sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE approvals SET apply_state = 'skipped' WHERE id = ? AND status = 'approved' \
             AND (apply_state IS NULL OR apply_state = 'queued')",
        )
        .bind(approval_id)
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(res.rows_affected() == 1)
    }
}
```

**Create `crates/dots-core/src/server/browser.rs`:**

```rust
//! The built-in browser of a Claude-engine run (browser spec section 2.2): `POST
//! /mcp/s/browser` is one MCP server for the CLI. It lists the pinned Playwright tools without
//! starting anything, and hands each call to the run's browser, whose gate decides it
//! (`RunBrowser::call`). A call that is not performed is a tool result with `isError`.

use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use axum::response::{IntoResponse, Response};
use axum::Json;
use serde_json::{json, Value};

use super::gateway::cli_limit;
use super::mcp::{initialize_result, read_request, rpc_error};
use super::ServerState;
use crate::browser::{self, RunBrowser};

pub(super) async fn handle(
    s: &ServerState,
    run_id: &str,
    browser: &RunBrowser,
    body: &[u8],
) -> Response {
    let started = Instant::now();
    let (id, req) = match read_request(body) {
        Ok(read) => read,
        Err(answer) => return *answer,
    };
    let method = req
        .get("method")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let result = match method {
        "initialize" => initialize_result(&req, browser::SERVER),
        "ping" => json!({}),
        "tools/list" => json!({ "tools": browser::tools() }),
        "tools/call" => {
            let Some(params) = req.get("params") else {
                return rpc_error(id, -32602, "missing params");
            };
            let Some(tool) = params.get("name").and_then(Value::as_str) else {
                return rpc_error(id, -32602, "params.name is required");
            };
            let input = match params.get("arguments") {
                None | Some(Value::Null) => json!({}),
                Some(args @ Value::Object(_)) => args.clone(),
                Some(_) => return rpc_error(id, -32602, "params.arguments must be an object"),
            };
            let limit = cli_limit(s, run_id)
                .await
                .saturating_sub(started.elapsed())
                .max(Duration::from_secs(1));
            let cancel = s.runner.run_cancel(run_id).unwrap_or_default();
            browser
                .call(s.hub.as_ref(), tool, input, limit, &cancel)
                .await
        }
        other => return rpc_error(id, -32601, &format!("method not found: {other}")),
    };
    Json(json!({ "jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": id, "result": result })).into_response()
}
```

### Task 8: The `## Browser` section of the first prompt

A thread's first prompt of a dot with `browser` on gains the section of spec section 4, after the standing rules and before the shared context. Resumes, and chats and follow-ups that resume a session, send only their message, as before.

**Files:**

- Modify: `crates/dots-core/src/browser/mod.rs` (append)
- Modify: `crates/dots-core/src/prompt.rs` (`build_prompt`, after the standing rules)
- Test: `crates/dots-core/tests/engine_prompt_test.rs` (append)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `prompt::build_prompt`; `DotSpec.browser` (Task 1).
- Produces: `pub const PROMPT: &str` in `dots_core::browser`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

**Append to `crates/dots-core/tests/engine_prompt_test.rs`:**

```rust

#[test]
fn a_dot_with_the_browser_is_told_how_to_use_it() {
    use dots_core::browser::PROMPT;
    assert_eq!(
        PROMPT,
        "## Browser\n\n\
         You have a browser (tools named browser_*). It is a visible Microsoft Edge window with your\n\
         own profile; logins you were given stay there. Take a snapshot before you act, and act on the\n\
         references in it. Opening a site you have not used before, and actions such as paying,\n\
         sending, deleting or submitting, wait for the person's approval. Never type a password or a\n\
         code: when a page needs a login, 2FA or a CAPTCHA, call ask with \"Please <do it> in the browser\n\
         window, then answer done.\" Files you download land in downloads/ in your workspace.\n"
    );
    let mut dot = fake_dot("d", Path::new("C:/repo"), WorkspaceMode::Folder);
    let run = fake_run(TriggerKind::Manual, None, None);
    let ws = Path::new("C:/ws");
    assert!(!build_prompt(&dot, &run, ws, at(), "", &[]).contains("## Browser"));
    dot.spec.browser = true;
    let p = build_prompt(&dot, &run, ws, at(), "", &[]);
    assert!(
        p.ends_with(&format!("and what needs human review.\n\n{PROMPT}")),
        "{p}"
    );
    // Before the shared context.
    let p = build_prompt(&dot, &run, ws, at(), "", &collections());
    let browser = p.find("## Browser").unwrap();
    let shared = p.find("## Shared context").unwrap();
    assert!(browser < shared, "{p}");
    assert!(p.contains(&format!("{PROMPT}\n## Shared context")), "{p}");
    // A resume sends only its message.
    let resume = fake_run(
        TriggerKind::Resume,
        Some(json!({"message": "Go on."})),
        Some("s1"),
    );
    assert_eq!(build_prompt(&dot, &resume, ws, at(), "", &[]), "Go on.");
}
```

```rust
    out.push_str(
        "- End with a short summary: what you did, what changed, and what needs human review.\n",
    );

```

**with:**

```rust
    out.push_str(
        "- End with a short summary: what you did, what changed, and what needs human review.\n",
    );
    // A dot with a browser is told how to use it (browser spec section 4).
    if dot.spec.browser {
        out.push('\n');
        out.push_str(crate::browser::PROMPT);
    }
    let section = context::prompt_section(contexts, context_tool_name(dot.spec.engine));
```

In a Rust string literal a `\` at the end of a line drops the line break and the next line's leading spaces; each line above therefore ends with `\n\` to keep its own break, and the text matches the spec's line by line.

### Task 9: The app's commands, the Edge path's confirmation, and the contract

The runtime gains "Open the browser" (`browser_open`), the status Settings shows (`browser_status`), and the screenshot reader of the run view (`browser_screenshot`, which reads only a file Playwright named in the run's `downloads/`); deleting a dot closes its Edge and removes its profile. The app registers the three commands; a new Edge path is confirmed in a native dialog like a new Claude program; the contract gains `BrowserStatus`, and the UI's schema and wrappers follow.

**Files:**

- Modify: `crates/dots-core/src/runtime.rs` (the `browsers` field, `delete_dot`; a block appended)
- Modify: `crates/dots-core/src/browser/mod.rs` (append)
- Test: `crates/dots-core/tests/runtime_browser_test.rs` (new)
- Modify: `apps/dots-app/src/commands.rs` (imports, `update_settings`; three commands appended)
- Modify: `apps/dots-app/src/lib.rs` (`command_list!`)
- Modify: `apps/dots-app/src/confirm.rs` (`save_settings_confirmed`, the Edge question, the tests' `save` and a new test)
- Modify: `apps/dots-app/src/contract.rs` (import, `BrowserStatus` sample)
- Modify: `ui/src/api/types.ts`, `ui/src/api/commands.ts`, `ui/src/api/commands.test.ts`, `ui/src/api/contract.test.ts`
- Regenerate: `ui/src/api/__fixtures__/contract.json`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `Browsers::{open_for_person, status, forget}` (Task 6); `Runtime::run_folder`; `confirm::{claude_program_to_confirm, ask}`.
- Produces:
  - `Runtime::browser_open(&self, dot_id: &str) -> Result<()>`, `Runtime::browser_status(&self) -> BrowserStatus`, `Runtime::browser_screenshot(&self, run_id: &str, name: &str) -> Result<String>`; `Runtime::delete_dot` also calls `Browsers::forget`.
  - `browser::screenshot(workspace: &Path, name: &str) -> crate::Result<String>`, `browser::MAX_SCREENSHOT_BYTES` (16 MiB).
  - Commands `browser_open(dot_id)`, `browser_status()`, `browser_screenshot(run_id, name)`.
  - `confirm::{CLAUDE_TITLE, EDGE_TITLE, edge_program_message}`; `save_settings_confirmed`'s `ask` becomes `FnMut(&'static str, String) -> Fut` (title, question).

**Create `crates/dots-core/tests/runtime_browser_test.rs`:**

```rust
//! The runtime's browser commands (browser spec sections 6 to 8), with `fake-browser` as Edge:
//! "Open the browser" opens the dot's Edge on its profile and a second click finds it; the
//! status names Edge; a deleted dot's Edge closes and its profile goes; a run's screenshot is
//! read back as a `data:` URL, only under the name Playwright gives one.

mod common;

use std::time::Duration;

use common::fake_browser::{exe, FakeEdges};
use common::spec;
use dots_core::model::{NewRun, TriggerKind, WorkspaceMode};
use dots_core::settings::Settings;
use dots_core::{Config, Error, Runtime};

/// A runtime on a temporary data folder, with no Claude CLI and `fake-browser` as Edge.
async fn runtime(dir: &tempfile::TempDir) -> Runtime {
    let mut cfg = Config::new(dir.path().join("data"));
    cfg.port = 0;
    cfg.claude_path = Some(dir.path().join("no-claude.exe"));
    let rt = Runtime::start(cfg).await.unwrap();
    let settings = Settings {
        edge_path: Some(exe()),
        ..rt.settings().await
    };
    rt.update_settings(settings).await.unwrap();
    rt
}

fn browser_spec(dir: &tempfile::TempDir) -> dots_core::model::DotSpec {
    let mut s = spec("shopper");
    s.workdir = dir.path().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
    s.workspace_mode = WorkspaceMode::Folder;
    s.browser = true;
    s
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn open_the_browser_opens_the_dots_edge_and_deleting_the_dot_removes_it() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let rt = runtime(&dir).await;
    let root = dir.path().join("data").join("browser");
    let edges = FakeEdges::new(&root);
    let dot = rt.create_dot(browser_spec(&dir)).await.unwrap();
    rt.browser_open(&dot.id).await.unwrap();
    assert!(edges.running(&dot.id));
    let args: Vec<String> =
        serde_json::from_value(edges.starts(&dot.id)[0]["args"].clone()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        args[0],
        format!("--user-data-dir={}", edges.profile(&dot.id).display())
    );
    rt.browser_open(&dot.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(edges.starts(&dot.id).len(), 1, "a second click finds it");
    assert!(matches!(
        rt.browser_open("no-such-dot").await,
        Err(Error::NotFound(_))
    ));
    rt.delete_dot(&dot.id).await.unwrap();
    assert!(edges.gone_within(&dot.id, Duration::from_secs(10)).await);
    assert!(!root.join(&dot.id).exists(), "the profile goes with its dot");
    rt.shutdown();
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_status_names_edge_and_a_missing_edge_is_said() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let rt = runtime(&dir).await;
    let status = rt.browser_status().await;
    assert_eq!(status.edge.as_deref(), Some(exe().as_str()));
    assert!(status
        .npx
        .is_none_or(|npx| npx.to_ascii_lowercase().contains("npx")));
    let missing = dir.path().join("no-edge.exe").to_string_lossy().into_owned();
    let settings = Settings {
        edge_path: Some(missing),
        ..rt.settings().await
    };
    rt.update_settings(settings).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(rt.browser_status().await.edge, None);
    let dot = rt.create_dot(browser_spec(&dir)).await.unwrap();
    let err = rt.browser_open(&dot.id).await.unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(
        err.to_string(),
        "Microsoft Edge was not found; browser use needs it (Settings → Edge path)."
    );
    rt.shutdown();
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_screenshot_is_read_only_by_its_playwright_name() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let rt = runtime(&dir).await;
    let dot = rt.create_dot(browser_spec(&dir)).await.unwrap();
    let ws = dir.path().join("ws");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(ws.join("downloads")).unwrap();
    let mut new = NewRun::new(&dot.id, TriggerKind::Manual);
    new.workspace_path = Some(ws.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
    let run = rt.store().create_run(&new).await.unwrap();
    let name = "page-2026-10-21T12-16-35-575Z.png";
    std::fs::write(ws.join("downloads").join(name), [0x89, b'P', b'N', b'G']).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        rt.browser_screenshot(&run.id, name).await.unwrap(),
        "data:image/png;base64,iVBORw=="
    );
    for other in [
        "../page-1.png",
        "page-1.png/../../secret",
        "statement.pdf",
        "page-.png",
        "page-1.gif",
        "PAGE-1.png",
        "page-1.png.exe",
    ] {
        assert!(
            matches!(rt.browser_screenshot(&run.id, other).await, Err(Error::Invalid(_))),
            "{other}"
        );
    }
    assert!(matches!(
        rt.browser_screenshot(&run.id, "page-2026-10-21T12-16-35-575Z.jpeg")
            .await,
        Err(Error::NotFound(_))
    ));
    rt.shutdown();
}
```

```rust
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn a_new_edge_program_is_confirmed_and_a_declined_one_is_not_saved() {
        const EDGE: &str = "D:/Apps/Edge/msedge.exe";
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let rt = runtime(&dir).await;
        let before = rt.settings().await;
        let edge = |path: Option<&str>| Settings {
            edge_path: path.map(str::to_string),
            ..before.clone()
        };

        let (result, asked) = save(&rt, edge(Some(EDGE)), false).await;
        assert_eq!(
            result,
            Err(CmdError::cancelled("the Edge program was not changed"))
        );
        assert_eq!(asked, [edge_program_message(EDGE)]);
        assert_eq!(rt.settings().await, before);

        // Allowed: trimmed once, saved as shown, and used from the next start of an Edge.
        let typed = format!(" {EDGE} ");
        let (result, asked) = save(&rt, edge(Some(typed.as_str())), true).await;
        let saved = result.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(asked, [edge_program_message(EDGE)]);
        assert_eq!(saved.settings.edge_path.as_deref(), Some(EDGE));
        assert!(!saved.restart_required);

        // The same program, or none: no question.
        let (result, asked) = save(&rt, saved.settings.clone(), false).await;
        assert!(result.is_ok());
        assert_eq!(asked, [] as [String; 0]);
        let (result, asked) = save(&rt, edge(None), false).await;
        assert_eq!(result.unwrap().settings.edge_path, None);
        assert_eq!(asked, [] as [String; 0]);
        rt.shutdown();
    }
}
```

**Append to `crates/dots-core/src/browser/mod.rs`:**

```rust

/// The largest screenshot the run view shows.
pub const MAX_SCREENSHOT_BYTES: u64 = 16 * 1024 * 1024;

/// A screenshot the run's browser saved into `workspace/downloads` under the name Playwright
/// gives one (`page-2026-10-21T12-16-35-575Z.png`), as a `data:` URL (browser spec section 7).
/// Any other name is `Invalid`: no other file can be read through it.
pub fn screenshot(workspace: &Path, name: &str) -> crate::Result<String> {
    use base64::Engine as _;
    let kind = screenshot_kind(name).ok_or_else(|| {
        crate::Error::Invalid(format!("{name:?} is not a screenshot of the browser"))
    })?;
    let path = workspace.join("downloads").join(name);
    let size = std::fs::metadata(&path)
        .map_err(|_| crate::Error::NotFound(format!("screenshot {name}")))?
        .len();
    if size > MAX_SCREENSHOT_BYTES {
        return Err(crate::Error::Invalid(format!(
            "screenshot {name} is too large to show"
        )));
    }
    let bytes = std::fs::read(&path)?;
    Ok(format!(
        "data:image/{kind};base64,{}",
        base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(bytes)
    ))
}

/// The image type of a name Playwright gives a screenshot: `page-<digits, '-', 'T', 'Z'>`
/// and `.png`, `.jpeg` or `.webp`.
fn screenshot_kind(name: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
    let (stem, ext) = name.rsplit_once('.')?;
    let kind = match ext {
        "png" => "png",
        "jpeg" => "jpeg",
        "webp" => "webp",
        _ => return None,
    };
    let time = stem.strip_prefix("page-")?;
    let plain = !time.is_empty()
        && time
            .chars()
            .all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '-' || c == 'T' || c == 'Z');
    plain.then_some(kind)
}
```

**Append to `crates/dots-core/src/runtime.rs`:**

```rust

/// Browser use (browser spec sections 7 and 8).
impl Runtime {
    /// "Open the browser": the dot's Edge, for the person to log in ahead of time. It stays open
    /// until they close it, and the dot's runs use it meanwhile.
    pub async fn browser_open(&self, dot_id: &str) -> Result<()> {
        self.store.get_dot(dot_id).await?;
        self.browsers
            .open_for_person(dot_id)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| Error::Other(e.to_string()))
    }

    /// Where Microsoft Edge and Node.js (`npx`) were found, as a run would start them.
    pub async fn browser_status(&self) -> crate::browser::BrowserStatus {
        self.browsers.status().await
    }

    /// A screenshot the run's browser saved in its workspace's `downloads/`, by the name
    /// Playwright gave it, as a `data:` URL.
    pub async fn browser_screenshot(&self, run_id: &str, name: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let folder = self.run_folder(run_id).await?;
        crate::browser::screenshot(&folder, name)
    }
}
```

```rust
/// The title of the question about a new Claude program.
pub const CLAUDE_TITLE: &str = "Change the Claude program?";
/// The title of the question about a new Edge program (browser spec section 6).
pub const EDGE_TITLE: &str = "Change the Edge program?";

/// The question about a new Edge program: Dots starts it for every dot that uses the browser.
pub fn edge_program_message(program: &str) -> String {
    format!(
        "Dots will run this program as Microsoft Edge for the dots that use the browser:\n\n\
         {program}\n\nAllow this only for a program you trust."
    )
}
```

**with:**

```rust
    mut ask: F,
) -> Result<SettingsSaved, CmdError>
where
    F: FnMut(&'static str, String) -> Fut,
    Fut: Future<Output = bool>,
{
    settings.claude_path = settings.claude_path.map(|p| p.trim().to_string());
    settings.edge_path = settings.edge_path.map(|p| p.trim().to_string());
    settings.validate()?;
    let stored = rt.settings().await;
    if let Some(program) = claude_program_to_confirm(
        stored.claude_path.as_deref(),
        settings.claude_path.as_deref(),
    ) {
        if !ask(CLAUDE_TITLE, claude_program_message(&program)).await {
            return Err(CmdError::cancelled("the Claude program was not changed"));
        }
    }
    // The same rule for the Edge program: only a new, non-empty path asks.
    if let Some(program) = claude_program_to_confirm(
        stored.edge_path.as_deref(),
        settings.edge_path.as_deref(),
    ) {
        if !ask(EDGE_TITLE, edge_program_message(&program)).await {
            return Err(CmdError::cancelled("the Edge program was not changed"));
        }
    }
    Ok(rt.update_settings(settings).await?)
```

```rust
/// "Open the browser" (browser spec section 7): the dot's Edge, for the person to log in ahead
/// of time. It stays open until they close it.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn browser_open(state: State<'_, AppState>, dot_id: String) -> Res<()> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.browser_open(&dot_id).await?)
}

/// Where Microsoft Edge and Node.js (`npx`) were found, for Settings.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn browser_status(state: State<'_, AppState>) -> Res<BrowserStatus> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.browser_status().await)
}

/// A screenshot of a run's browser as a `data:` URL: only a file Playwright named in the run's
/// `downloads/` folder (`page-<time>.png`), so the UI cannot read any other file.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn browser_screenshot(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    run_id: String,
    name: String,
) -> Res<String> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.browser_screenshot(&run_id, &name).await?)
}
```

### Task 10: The editor's Browser section and the Settings status

`ui/src/lib/browser.ts` holds what the screens know of browser use: the sites a policy approves (its allow rules `Browser(<site>)` and `BrowserRisky(<site>)`) and their removal, the card's sentence of a browser approval, the run-view line of a browser call and the screenshot a result names (the last three used in Task 11). The dot editor gains a "Browser" section: the switch, the approved sites (each with "risky actions allowed" when set, and Remove) and "Open the browser" for a stored dot. Settings gains a "Browser" section: whether Microsoft Edge and Node.js were found, and the Edge path, whose declined confirmation is said under the field.

- Create: `ui/src/lib/browser.ts`, `ui/src/lib/browser.test.ts`
- Create: `ui/src/screens/dot-editor/BrowserSection.tsx`
- Modify: `ui/src/screens/dot-editor/DotForm.tsx` (imports; a section after "Tools and skills")

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `commands.browserOpen`, `commands.browserStatus` (Task 9); `DotSpec.browser`, `Settings.edge_path` (Task 1).
- Produces (in `ui/src/lib/browser.ts`): `BROWSER_PREFIX`; `type Site = { site: string; risky: boolean }`; `sitesOf(policy: Policy): Site[]`; `withoutSite(policy: Policy, site: string): Policy`; `type BrowserAsk`; `browserAsk(approval: Approval): BrowserAsk | null`; `type Part = { text: string; strong: boolean }`; `askParts(ask: BrowserAsk): Part[]`; `askText(ask: BrowserAsk): string`; `browserLine(tool: string, input: unknown, result: { output: string; isError: boolean } | null): string`; `screenshotName(output: string): string | null`. Components `BrowserSection({ spec, existing, onChange })`, `BrowserSettings({ edgePath, onEdgePath, declined })`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Action, Approval, Policy } from "@/api/types";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";

import {
  askText,
  browserAsk,
  browserLine,
  screenshotName,
  sitesOf,
  withoutSite,
  type BrowserAsk,
} from "./browser";

const policy = (rules: [string, Action][]): Policy => ({
  ...sample.spec.policy,
  preset: "sandboxed",
  rules: rules.map(([tool, action]) => ({ tool, action })),
});

describe("the sites a dot's rules approve", () => {
  it("lists the allowed sites with their risky actions, and removes one", () => {
    const p = policy([
      ["Read", "allow"],
      ["Browser(shop.com)", "allow"],
      ["BrowserRisky(shop.com)", "allow"],
      ["BrowserRisky(pay.example)", "allow"],
      ["Browser(news.example)", "allow"],
      ["Browser(bank.com)", "deny"],
      ["Browser(ask.example)", "ask"],
    ]);
    expect(sitesOf(p)).toEqual([
      { site: "news.example", risky: false },
      { site: "pay.example", risky: true },
      { site: "shop.com", risky: true },
    ]);
    const removed = withoutSite(p, "shop.com");
    expect(removed.rules.map((r) => r.tool)).toEqual([
      "Read",
      "BrowserRisky(pay.example)",
      "Browser(news.example)",
      "Browser(bank.com)",
      "Browser(ask.example)",
    ]);
    expect(removed.preset).toBe("custom");
    expect(withoutSite(p, "other.example")).toBe(p);
  });
});

const ask = (over: Partial<BrowserAsk>): BrowserAsk => ({
  action: "click",
  site: "shop.com",
  page: "shop.com/cart",
  labels: ["Pay now"],
  risky: true,
  dangerous: false,
  ...over,
});

describe("a browser approval's card", () => {
  it("says what and where, in the spec's words", () => {
    expect(askText(ask({ action: "open", labels: [], risky: false }))).toBe(
      "wants to open shop.com/cart",
    );
    expect(askText(ask({}))).toBe('wants to click "Pay now" on shop.com');
    expect(askText(ask({ action: "type", labels: ["Search"], risky: false }))).toBe(
      'wants to type into "Search" on shop.com',
    );
    expect(askText(ask({ labels: [null] }))).toBe(
      "wants to click an element Dots could not identify on shop.com",
    );
    expect(askText(ask({ site: null, page: "about:blank" }))).toBe(
      'wants to click "Pay now" on a page with no site',
    );
    expect(askText(ask({ action: "fill", labels: ["Search", "Email"] }))).toBe(
      'wants to fill in "Search", "Email" on shop.com',
    );
    expect(askText(ask({ action: "press", labels: ["Enter"] }))).toBe(
      "wants to press Enter on shop.com",
    );
    expect(askText(ask({ action: "drag", labels: ["Item", "Bin"] }))).toBe(
      'wants to drag "Item" to "Bin" on shop.com',
    );
    expect(askText(ask({ action: "dialog", labels: [] }))).toBe(
      "wants to accept a dialog on shop.com",
    );
    expect(askText(ask({ action: "run_code", labels: [], dangerous: true }))).toBe(
      "wants to run its own code in the page on shop.com",
    );
    expect(askText(ask({ action: "other", labels: ["checkout_now"], dangerous: true }))).toBe(
      "wants to use checkout_now on shop.com",
    );
  });

  it("is read only from a browser tool's card", () => {
    const card = ask({});
    const browser: Approval = {
      ...sample.approval,
      tool: "mcp__browser__browser_click",
      input: { target: "e16", dots_browser: card },
    };
    expect(browserAsk(browser)).toEqual(card);
    expect(browserAsk({ ...browser, tool: "mcp__files__write" })).toBeNull();
    expect(browserAsk({ ...browser, input: { target: "e16" } })).toBeNull();
    expect(browserAsk(sample.approval)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("a browser call in the run view", () => {
  const done = (output: string) => ({ output, isError: false });

  it("is the lines Dots put in front of its result", () => {
    expect(
      browserLine(
        "mcp__browser__browser_navigate",
        { url: "https://shop.com/cart" },
        done("Dots: Opened shop.com/cart\n### Ran Playwright code"),
      ),
    ).toBe("Opened shop.com/cart");
    expect(
      browserLine(
        "mcp__browser__browser_click",
        { target: "e5" },
        done('Dots: Clicked "Add to cart"\nDots: Downloaded statement.pdf\n### Page'),
      ),
    ).toBe('Clicked "Add to cart" · Downloaded statement.pdf');
    // A line further down is the page's, not Dots'.
    expect(
      browserLine("mcp__browser__browser_click", { target: "e5" }, done("### Page\nDots: Paid")),
    ).toBe("Click");
    expect(
      browserLine("mcp__browser__browser_navigate", { url: "https://shop.com/cart" }, null),
    ).toBe("Open https://shop.com/cart");
    expect(
      browserLine("mcp__browser__browser_evaluate", {}, { output: "Denied", isError: true }),
    ).toBe("Use browser_evaluate");
  });

  it("finds the screenshot its result names", () => {
    expect(
      screenshotName(
        "### Result\n- [Screenshot of viewport](downloads\\page-2026-10-21T12-16-35-575Z.png)",
      ),
    ).toBe("page-2026-10-21T12-16-35-575Z.png");
    expect(screenshotName("- [Snapshot](./snap.md)")).toBeNull();
    expect(screenshotName("(downloads/../secret.png)")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/BrowserEditor.test.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Dot, DotSpec } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

const dot: Dot = {
  ...sample.dot,
  browser: true,
  policy: {
    ...sample.dot.policy,
    rules: [
      ...sample.dot.policy.rules,
      { tool: "Browser(shop.com)", action: "allow" },
      { tool: "BrowserRisky(shop.com)", action: "allow" },
      { tool: "Browser(news.example)", action: "allow" },
      { tool: "Browser(bank.com)", action: "deny" },
    ],
  },
};

describe("the dot editor's browser", () => {
  it("lists the approved sites, removes one, and opens the browser", async () => {
    // Assigned in the handler: declared so, TypeScript does not narrow it to null.
    let saved = null as DotSpec | null;
    backend.on("get_dot", () => dot);
    backend.on("update_dot", (args) => {
      saved = args.spec as DotSpec;
      return { ...dot, ...saved };
    });
    backend.on("browser_open", () => null);
    const { user } = renderApp(`/dots/${dot.id}`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("switch", { name: "Browser" })).toBeChecked();
    expect(
      screen.getByText("Opens a visible Microsoft Edge window with this dot's own profile."),
    ).toBeVisible();
    const sites = screen.getByRole("list", { name: "Sites" });
    const shop = within(sites).getByRole("listitem", { name: "shop.com" });
    expect(shop).toHaveTextContent("risky actions allowed");
    expect(within(sites).getByRole("listitem", { name: "news.example" })).not.toHaveTextContent(
      "risky actions allowed",
    );
    // A denied site is not an approved one.
    expect(within(sites).queryByRole("listitem", { name: "bank.com" })).toBeNull();

    await user.click(within(shop).getByRole("button", { name: "Remove" }));
    expect(within(sites).queryByRole("listitem", { name: "shop.com" })).toBeNull();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Open the browser" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(backend.callsTo("browser_open")).toEqual([{ dotId: dot.id }]));

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(saved).not.toBeNull());
    const tools = saved!.policy.rules.map((r) => r.tool);
    expect(saved!.browser).toBe(true);
    expect(tools).not.toContain("Browser(shop.com)");
    expect(tools).not.toContain("BrowserRisky(shop.com)");
    expect(tools).toContain("Browser(news.example)");
    expect(tools).toContain("Browser(bank.com)");
    expect(saved!.policy.preset).toBe("custom");
  });

  it("turns the browser on for a new dot, whose browser opens once it is saved", async () => {
    const { user } = renderApp("/dots/new");
    const toggle = await screen.findByRole("switch", { name: "Browser" });
    expect(toggle).not.toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("heading", { name: "Sites" })).toBeNull();
    await user.click(toggle);
    expect(toggle).toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "Sites" })).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText(/^No site is approved yet/)).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText("Save the dot to open its browser.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Open the browser" })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Settings } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

describe("browser use in Settings", () => {
  it("says what was found and saves the Edge path", async () => {
    let current: Settings = sample.settings;
    backend.on("get_settings", () => current);
    backend.on("browser_status", () => ({ edge: "C:/Edge/msedge.exe", npx: null }));
    backend.on("update_settings", (args) => {
      current = args.settings as Settings;
      return { settings: current, restart_required: false };
    });
    const { user } = renderApp("/settings");
    expect(await screen.findByText("Microsoft Edge found")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText("C:/Edge/msedge.exe")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText("Node.js not found")).toBeVisible();
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Edge path"), "  D:/Edge/msedge.exe ");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(backend.callsTo("update_settings")).toEqual([
        { settings: { ...sample.settings, edge_path: "D:/Edge/msedge.exe" } },
      ]),
    );
  });

  it("says so under the field when the Edge program was declined", async () => {
    backend.on("update_settings", () => {
      throw { kind: "cancelled", message: "the Edge program was not changed" };
    });
    const { user } = renderApp("/settings");
    expect(await screen.findByText("Microsoft Edge not found")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText("Node.js not found")).toBeVisible();
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Edge path"), "D:/Edge/msedge.exe");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
    expect(
      await screen.findByText("Not saved: you declined the change of the Edge program."),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(
      screen.queryByText("Not saved: you declined the change of the Claude program."),
    ).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/lib/browser.ts`:**

```ts
// Browser use (browser spec): the sites a dot's rules approve, what a browser approval asks,
// and the run-view line of a browser call.
import { z } from "zod";

import type { Approval, Policy } from "@/api/types";

/// The built-in browser's tools are `mcp__browser__<tool>` on both engines.
export const BROWSER_PREFIX = "mcp__browser__";

/// A site the dot may act on without asking, and whether its risky actions are allowed too.
export type Site = { site: string; risky: boolean };

const SITE_RULE = /^(Browser|BrowserRisky)\((.+)\)$/;

/// The site of a rule `Browser(<site>)` or `BrowserRisky(<site>)`, and which of the two it is.
function siteRule(tool: string): Site | null {
  const match = SITE_RULE.exec(tool.trim());
  return match ? { site: match[2]!.trim(), risky: match[1] === "BrowserRisky" } : null;
}

/// The sites a policy approves, by name: its allow rules `Browser(<site>)` and
/// `BrowserRisky(<site>)` (an allowed `BrowserRisky` approves the site too).
export function sitesOf(policy: Policy): Site[] {
  const sites = new Map<string, boolean>();
  for (const rule of policy.rules) {
    const parsed = rule.action === "allow" ? siteRule(rule.tool) : null;
    if (parsed) sites.set(parsed.site, (sites.get(parsed.site) ?? false) || parsed.risky);
  }
  return [...sites]
    .map(([site, risky]) => ({ site, risky }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.site.localeCompare(b.site));
}

/// The policy without the allow rules of `site`. The preset becomes custom when a rule goes, as
/// when a rule is saved from an approval; the policy is returned as it is otherwise.
export function withoutSite(policy: Policy, site: string): Policy {
  const rules = policy.rules.filter(
    (rule) => !(rule.action === "allow" && siteRule(rule.tool)?.site === site),
  );
  return rules.length === policy.rules.length ? policy : { ...policy, preset: "custom", rules };
}

/// What Dots stored with a browser call that asks: `dots_browser` in the approval's input.
const BrowserAsk = z.object({
  action: z.string(),
  site: z.string().nullable(),
  page: z.string().nullable(),
  labels: z.array(z.string().nullable()),
  risky: z.boolean(),
  dangerous: z.boolean(),
});
export type BrowserAsk = z.infer<typeof BrowserAsk>;

/// The card of a browser approval; null for any other approval.
export function browserAsk(approval: Approval): BrowserAsk | null {
  if (!approval.tool.startsWith(BROWSER_PREFIX)) return null;
  const input = approval.input;
  if (typeof input !== "object" || input === null) return null;
  const parsed = BrowserAsk.safeParse((input as Record<string, unknown>).dots_browser);
  return parsed.success ? parsed.data : null;
}

/// A part of a card's sentence: plain, or in bold.
export type Part = { text: string; strong: boolean };

const plain = (text: string): Part => ({ text, strong: false });
const strong = (text: string): Part => ({ text, strong: true });

/// An element by its label in quotes; one Dots could not identify says so.
const quoted = (label: string | null | undefined) =>
  label === null || label === undefined ? "an element Dots could not identify" : `"${label}"`;

/// What the dot wants to do, as the card says it (browser spec section 3.6): "wants to open
/// **shop.com/cart**", "wants to click **"Pay now"** on **shop.com**".
export function askParts(ask: BrowserAsk): Part[] {
  const where =
    ask.site === null ? [plain(" on a page with no site")] : [plain(" on "), strong(ask.site)];
  const [first, second] = ask.labels;
  switch (ask.action) {
    case "open":
      return [plain("wants to open "), strong(ask.page ?? "a page")];
    case "click":
      return [plain("wants to click "), strong(quoted(first)), ...where];
    case "type":
      return [plain("wants to type into "), strong(quoted(first)), ...where];
    case "fill":
      return [plain("wants to fill in "), strong(ask.labels.map(quoted).join(", ")), ...where];
    case "select":
      return [plain("wants to choose an option in "), strong(quoted(first)), ...where];
    case "press":
      return [plain("wants to press "), strong(first ?? "a key"), ...where];
    case "hover":
      return [plain("wants to point at "), strong(quoted(first)), ...where];
    case "drag":
      return [
        plain("wants to drag "),
        strong(quoted(first)),
        plain(" to "),
        strong(quoted(second)),
        ...where,
      ];
    case "drop":
      return [plain("wants to drop data onto "), strong(quoted(first)), ...where];
    case "dialog":
      return [plain("wants to accept a dialog"), ...where];
    case "run_code":
      return [plain("wants to run its own code in the page"), ...where];
    case "upload":
      return [plain("wants to upload files from this computer"), ...where];
    case "network":
      return [plain("wants to read the page's network requests"), ...where];
    default:
      return [plain("wants to use "), strong(first ?? ask.action), ...where];
  }
}

/// The card's sentence as plain text.
export function askText(ask: BrowserAsk): string {
  return askParts(ask)
    .map((part) => part.text)
    .join("");
}

/// The trimmed string argument `key` of a call, or "".
function field(input: unknown, key: string): string {
  const value =
    typeof input === "object" && input !== null
      ? (input as Record<string, unknown>)[key]
      : undefined;
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

/// The run-view line of a browser call (browser spec section 7): the lines Dots put in front of
/// its result (`Dots: Opened shop.com/cart`), joined; before its result, or for a call that was
/// not performed, what the call is.
export function browserLine(
  tool: string,
  input: unknown,
  result: { output: string; isError: boolean } | null,
): string {
  const reported: string[] = [];
  for (const line of (result?.output ?? "").split("\n")) {
    if (!line.startsWith("Dots: ")) break;
    reported.push(line.slice("Dots: ".length));
  }
  if (reported.length > 0) return reported.join(" · ");
  const name = tool.startsWith(BROWSER_PREFIX) ? tool.slice(BROWSER_PREFIX.length) : tool;
  const url = field(input, "url");
  switch (name) {
    case "browser_navigate":
      return url ? `Open ${url}` : "Open a page";
    case "browser_snapshot":
      return "Read the page";
    case "browser_take_screenshot":
      return "Take a screenshot";
    case "browser_click":
      return "Click";
    case "browser_type":
      return "Type";
    default:
      return `Use ${name}`;
  }
}

/// The screenshot a result names, by its file name: Playwright saves one as
/// `downloads\page-<time>.png` in the run's workspace.
export function screenshotName(output: string): string | null {
  const match = /\(downloads[\\/](page-[0-9TZ-]+\.(?:png|jpeg|webp))\)/.exec(output);
  return match ? match[1]! : null;
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/dot-editor/BrowserSection.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { Dot, DotSpec } from "@/api/types";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { sitesOf, withoutSite } from "@/lib/browser";
import { notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";

/// The dot's browser (browser spec section 7): the switch, the sites it may act on without
/// asking, and, for a stored dot, "Open the browser" so the person can log in ahead of time.
/// Removing a site changes the form; Save stores it.
export function BrowserSection(props: {
  spec: DotSpec;
  existing: Dot | null;
  onChange: (spec: DotSpec) => void;
}) {
  const { spec, existing } = props;
  const open = useMutation({ mutationFn: commands.browserOpen, onError: notifyError });
  const sites = sitesOf(spec.policy);
  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Switch
          id="dot-browser"
          checked={spec.browser}
          onCheckedChange={(browser) => props.onChange({ ...spec, browser })}
        />
        <Label htmlFor="dot-browser">Browser</Label>
      </div>
      <p className="-mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        Opens a visible Microsoft Edge window with this dot's own profile.
      </p>
      {spec.browser && (
        <>
          <h3 id="dot-browser-sites" className="text-sm font-medium">
            Sites
          </h3>
          {sites.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              No site is approved yet: the dot asks before it opens a site or acts on one, and an
              approval can always allow the site.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <ul aria-labelledby="dot-browser-sites" className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              {sites.map(({ site, risky }) => (
                <li key={site} aria-label={site} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                  <span className="font-mono">{site}</span>
                  {risky && (
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">risky actions allowed</span>
                  )}
                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    size="sm"
                    variant="ghost"
                    className="ml-auto"
                    onClick={() =>
                      props.onChange({ ...spec, policy: withoutSite(spec.policy, site) })
                    }
                  >
                    Remove
                  </Button>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
          {existing ? (
            <div>
              <Button
                type="button"
                size="sm"
                variant="outline"
                disabled={open.isPending}
                onClick={() => open.mutate(existing.id)}
              >
                Open the browser
              </Button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Save the dot to open its browser.</p>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/components/BrowserSettings.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";

/// Browser use in Settings (browser spec section 7): whether Microsoft Edge and Node.js were
/// found, and the Edge path. A new path is confirmed in a system dialog when it is saved;
/// `declined` says that the person said no.
export function BrowserSettings(props: {
  edgePath: string;
  onEdgePath: (path: string) => void;
  declined: boolean;
}) {
  const status = useQuery({ queryKey: ["browser-status"], queryFn: commands.browserStatus });
  const edge = status.data?.edge ?? null;
  const npx = status.data?.npx ?? null;
  return (
    <>
      {status.data && (
        <ul aria-label="What browser use needs" className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs">
          <li>
            <span>{edge ? "Microsoft Edge found" : "Microsoft Edge not found"}</span>
            {edge && <span className="ml-2 font-mono text-muted-foreground">{edge}</span>}
          </li>
          <li>
            <span>{npx ? "Node.js found" : "Node.js not found"}</span>
            {npx && <span className="ml-2 font-mono text-muted-foreground">{npx}</span>}
          </li>
        </ul>
      )}
      <Field
        label="Edge path"
        htmlFor="settings-edge-path"
        error={props.declined ? "Not saved: you declined the change of the Edge program." : undefined}
        hint="Leave empty to use Microsoft Edge where it is installed. A new program must be confirmed in a system dialog."
      >
        <Input
          id="settings-edge-path"
          className="font-mono"
          value={props.edgePath}
          onChange={(e) => props.onEdgePath(e.target.value)}
        />
      </Field>
    </>
  );
}
```

### Task 11: The browser approval card and the run view's browser lines

A browser approval gets its own card: the dot's name and what it wants to do and where in the spec's words, a note on a dangerous call and on a parked one, and the buttons "Allow once", "Always allow on <site>", for a risky act "Always allow risky actions on <site>", and "Deny", the rules being the ones the core offers. In a transcript each `mcp__browser__*` call is one line, the `Dots:` lines of its result ("Opened shop.com/cart", "Clicked "Add to cart"", "Took a screenshot", "Downloaded statement.pdf"), and a screenshot it took is shown as an image.

**Files:**

- Create: `ui/src/components/BrowserApprovalCard.tsx`
- Modify: `ui/src/components/ApprovalCard.tsx` (imports; `ApprovalCard` chooses the card)
- Create: `ui/src/components/BrowserCall.tsx`
- Modify: `ui/src/lib/transcript.ts` (imports, `TranscriptItem`, `transcriptItems`)
- Modify: `ui/src/components/Transcript.tsx` (imports, `Item`, the transcript's items)
- Test: `ui/src/screens/BrowserApproval.test.tsx` (new), `ui/src/screens/RunBrowser.test.tsx` (new)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `browserAsk`, `askParts`, `browserLine`, `screenshotName`, `BROWSER_PREFIX` (Task 10); `commands.approvalRuleCandidates`, `commands.decideApproval`, `commands.browserScreenshot` (Task 9).
- Produces: `BrowserApprovalCard({ approval, ask, dotName })`, `BROWSER_PARKED`; `BrowserCall({ runId, tool, input, result, active })`; `TranscriptItem` gains `{ type: "browser"; seq; tool; input; result }`.

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Approval, PendingApproval } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

const card = {
  action: "click",
  site: "shop.com",
  page: "shop.com/cart",
  labels: ["Pay now"],
  risky: true,
  dangerous: false,
};
const pay: PendingApproval = {
  ...sample.inbox.approvals[0]!,
  tool: "mcp__browser__browser_click",
  input: { target: "e16", dots_browser: card },
};

function inbox(approvals: PendingApproval[]) {
  backend.on("inbox", () => ({ approvals, review_runs: [], attention: [], tools_changed: [] }));
  backend.on("decide_approval", (args): Approval => ({
    ...sample.approval,
    id: String(args.id),
    status: args.approved ? "approved" : "denied",
  }));
}

describe("a browser approval", () => {
  it("says what and where, and offers its site's rules", async () => {
    inbox([pay]);
    backend.on("approval_rule_candidates", () => [
      { pattern: "Browser(shop.com)", label: "always allow on shop.com" },
      { pattern: "BrowserRisky(shop.com)", label: "always allow risky actions on shop.com" },
    ]);
    const { user } = renderApp("/inbox");
    const article = await screen.findByRole("article", {
      name: `Browser approval for ${pay.dot_name}`,
    });
    expect(article).toHaveTextContent(`${pay.dot_name} wants to click "Pay now" on shop.com`);
    expect(within(article).getByText('"Pay now"').tagName).toBe("STRONG");
    expect(within(article).getByText("shop.com").tagName).toBe("STRONG");
    expect(within(article).getByRole("button", { name: "Allow once" })).toBeVisible();
    expect(
      await within(article).findByRole("button", { name: "Always allow on shop.com" }),
    ).toBeVisible();
    await user.click(
      within(article).getByRole("button", { name: "Always allow risky actions on shop.com" }),
    );
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(backend.callsTo("decide_approval")).toEqual([
        { id: pay.id, approved: true, note: null, allowRule: "BrowserRisky(shop.com)" },
      ]),
    );
  });

  it("asks to open a site, says a parked approval is not performed, and denies", async () => {
    const open: PendingApproval = {
      ...pay,
      id: "01J0APR2",
      tool: "mcp__browser__browser_navigate",
      parked: true,
      input: {
        url: "https://shop.com/cart",
        dots_browser: { ...card, action: "open", labels: [], risky: false },
      },
    };
    inbox([open]);
    backend.on("approval_rule_candidates", () => [
      { pattern: "Browser(shop.com)", label: "always allow on shop.com" },
    ]);
    const { user } = renderApp("/inbox");
    const article = await screen.findByRole("article", {
      name: `Browser approval for ${pay.dot_name}`,
    });
    expect(article).toHaveTextContent(`${pay.dot_name} wants to open shop.com/cart`);
    expect(article).toHaveTextContent(
      "The run stopped waiting. Approving now does not perform this action: the dot is told to take a snapshot and do it again if it is still right.",
    );
    expect(
      await within(article).findByRole("button", { name: "Always allow on shop.com" }),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(within(article).queryByRole("button", { name: /risky actions/ })).toBeNull();
    await user.click(within(article).getByRole("button", { name: "Deny" }));
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(backend.callsTo("decide_approval")).toEqual([
        { id: "01J0APR2", approved: false, note: null, allowRule: null },
      ]),
    );
  });

  it("keeps the tool card for every other call", async () => {
    inbox([sample.inbox.approvals[0]!]);
    renderApp("/inbox");
    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("article", { name: `Approval for ${sample.approval.tool}` }),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("article", { name: /^Browser approval/ })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Run, RunEventRecord } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

const run: Run = { ...sample.run, branch: null, base_commit: null };
let seq = 0;
const ev = (data: Record<string, unknown>): RunEventRecord => ({
  run_id: run.id,
  seq: ++seq,
  ts: run.queued_at,
  kind: String(data.kind),
  data,
});

describe("browser calls in the run view", () => {
  it("shows each call as one line, and a screenshot as an image", async () => {
    const events = [
      ev({
        kind: "tool_call",
        id: "b1",
        tool: "mcp__browser__browser_navigate",
        input: { url: "https://shop.com/cart" },
      }),
      ev({
        kind: "tool_result",
        id: "b1",
        output: "Dots: Opened shop.com/cart\n### Page\n- Page URL: https://shop.com/cart",
        is_error: false,
      }),
      ev({ kind: "tool_call", id: "b2", tool: "mcp__browser__browser_click", input: { target: "e5" } }),
      ev({
        kind: "tool_result",
        id: "b2",
        output: 'Dots: Clicked "Add to cart"\nDots: Downloaded statement.pdf\n### Page',
        is_error: false,
      }),
      ev({
        kind: "tool_call",
        id: "b3",
        tool: "mcp__browser__browser_type",
        input: { target: "e11", text: "shoes" },
      }),
      ev({ kind: "tool_result", id: "b3", output: 'Dots: Typed into "Search"\n### Page', is_error: false }),
      ev({
        kind: "tool_call",
        id: "b4",
        tool: "mcp__browser__browser_take_screenshot",
        input: { scale: "css" },
      }),
      ev({
        kind: "tool_result",
        id: "b4",
        output:
          "Dots: Took a screenshot\n### Result\n- [Screenshot of viewport](downloads\\page-2026-10-21T12-16-35-575Z.png)",
        is_error: false,
      }),
    ];
    backend.on("run_thread", () => [run]);
    backend.on("get_dot", () => sample.dot);
    backend.on("list_run_events", (args) =>
      events.filter((e) => e.seq > (args.afterSeq as number)),
    );
    backend.on("approvals_for_run", () => []);
    backend.on("worktree_diff", () => sample.diff);
    backend.on("browser_screenshot", () => "data:image/png;base64,iVBORw==");
    renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    expect(await screen.findByText("Opened shop.com/cart")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText('Clicked "Add to cart" · Downloaded statement.pdf')).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText('Typed into "Search"')).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText("Took a screenshot")).toBeVisible();
    const image = await screen.findByRole("img", { name: "Screenshot of the dot's browser" });
    expect(image).toHaveAttribute("src", "data:image/png;base64,iVBORw==");
    expect(backend.callsTo("browser_screenshot")).toEqual([
      { runId: run.id, name: "page-2026-10-21T12-16-35-575Z.png" },
    ]);
    // Not as raw tool calls.
    expect(screen.queryByText("browser › browser_click")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/components/BrowserApprovalCard.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Globe } from "lucide-react";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { Approval } from "@/api/types";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { askParts, type BrowserAsk } from "@/lib/browser";
import { notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, invalidateDots, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { onSingleClick } from "@/lib/single-click";

type Decision = { approved: boolean; allowRule: string | null };

/// What a parked browser card says: an approval decided now does not perform the call
/// (browser spec section 3.6).
export const BROWSER_PARKED =
  "The run stopped waiting. Approving now does not perform this action: the dot is told to take a snapshot and do it again if it is still right.";

/// A browser call that waits for the person (browser spec section 3.6): what the dot wants to
/// do and where, then Allow once, Always allow on <site>, for a risky act Always allow risky
/// actions on <site>, and Deny. The rules are the ones the core offers for this call; a
/// dangerous call is offered none.
export function BrowserApprovalCard({
  approval,
  ask,
  dotName,
}: {
  approval: Approval;
  ask: BrowserAsk;
  dotName?: string;
}) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const rules = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.approvalRules(approval.id),
    queryFn: () => commands.approvalRuleCandidates(approval.id),
  });
  const offered = rules.data ?? [];
  const site = offered.find((rule) => rule.pattern.startsWith("Browser("));
  const risky = offered.find((rule) => rule.pattern.startsWith("BrowserRisky("));
  const decide = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ approved, allowRule }: Decision) =>
      commands.decideApproval(approval.id, approved, null, allowRule),
    onSuccess: (_answer, { allowRule }) => {
      invalidate(qc, keys.inbox);
      invalidate(qc, keys.approvals(approval.run_id));
      // A saved rule changed the dot's policy, and no runtime event announces a dot change.
      if (allowRule !== null) invalidateDots(qc);
    },
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  // One decision per card, as on the tool card.
  const decided = decide.isPending || decide.isSuccess;
  const who = dotName ?? "The dot";
  const choose = (decision: Decision) => onSingleClick(() => decide.mutate(decision));
  return (
    <article
      aria-label={`Browser approval for ${who}`}
      className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10 p-3"
    >
      <p className="flex items-start gap-1.5">
        <Globe aria-hidden className="mt-1 size-3.5 shrink-0" />
        <span>
          <span className="font-semibold">{who}</span>{" "}
          {askParts(ask).map((part, i) =>
            part.strong ? <strong key={i}>{part.text}</strong> : <span key={i}>{part.text}</span>,
          )}
        </span>
      </p>
      {ask.dangerous && (
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No rule can allow this: it asks every time.</p>
      )}
      {approval.parked && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{BROWSER_PARKED}</p>}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <Button size="sm" disabled={decided} onClick={choose({ approved: true, allowRule: null })}>
          Allow once
        </Button>
        {site && ask.site && (
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            disabled={decided}
            onClick={choose({ approved: true, allowRule: site.pattern })}
          >
            Always allow on {ask.site}
          </Button>
        )}
        {risky && ask.site && (
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            disabled={decided}
            onClick={choose({ approved: true, allowRule: risky.pattern })}
          >
            Always allow risky actions on {ask.site}
          </Button>
        )}
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          disabled={decided}
          onClick={choose({ approved: false, allowRule: null })}
        >
          Deny
        </Button>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

```tsx
/// A call that waits for the user. A call of the built-in browser that Dots asked about has a
/// card of its own (`BrowserApprovalCard`); every other call has the tool card.
export function ApprovalCard(props: { approval: Approval; dotName?: string }) {
  const ask = browserAsk(props.approval);
  return ask ? (
    <BrowserApprovalCard approval={props.approval} ask={ask} dotName={props.dotName} />
  ) : (
    <ToolApprovalCard approval={props.approval} dotName={props.dotName} />
  );
}
```
